- **Authors**: Yi Wang, Corina Dima, Liangyu Zhong, Steffen Staab
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Entity Linking, Medical AI, Cross-Lingual
- **Summary**: 针对跨语言生物医学实体链接中低资源语言训练数据匮乏和SapBERT检索器泛化差的问题，提出BioELX两阶段框架。第一阶段用Wikidata多语言别名词汇增强SapBERT训练，第二阶段用预训练LLM进行上下文感知消歧。在五个基准上取得新SOTA，XL-BEL上Recall@1平均提升+19.2，低资源语言如土耳其语+21.6，韩语+22.1，泰语+30.8。

<h3 id="164">[164] The Computational Boundary of Inference: Capability Internalization, Training, and the Turing Jump</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27381](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27381) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chien-Ping Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computability Theory, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对AI递归自我改进中能力跃升的理论问题，用经典可计算性理论给出形式化分离结果。证明有限内部自修改保持在计算层内，而稳定修订由图灵跃迁控制。该结果对依赖重复内部更新实现定性能力提升的叙事提供了可计算性理论极限。

<h3 id="165">[165] The Alignment Floor: When Persona Customization Is Safe</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27382) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xing Zhang, Guanghui Wang, Yanwei Cui, Wei Qiu, Ziyuan Li, Bing Zhu, Peiyang He
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Persona Customization
- **Summary**: 针对个性化提示可能破坏模型对齐的问题，提出“对齐底线”概念。在强对齐模型上人格提示对谄媚行为无影响（~15%），而弱对齐模型上谄媚从5%升至50%。发现外向性和开放性比宜人性更危险，批判性思维提示可将谄媚降至5%。跨模型迁移几乎为零，提示对齐测试需逐模型进行。

<h3 id="166">[166] Bridging the Stability-Expressivity Gap: Synthetic Data Scaling and Preference Alignment for Low-Resource Spoken Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27383](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27383) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="167">[167] From Instructor to Collaborator: What a 90-Participant Study Reveals about Human-Agent Collaboration in a Mobile Serious Game</h3>

<h3 id="168">[168] Personalized Observation Normalization for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Simulation Environments with Heterogeneity</h3>

<h3 id="169">[169] From AR to Diffusion: Efficiently Adapting Large Language Models with Strictly Causal and Elastic Horizons</h3>

<h3 id="170">[170] Modeling Community Attitude through Reaction Tone: A Human-AI Collaborative Framework for Evaluating LLM Alignment with Linguistic Behaviors in Online Communities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27388) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="171">[171] Memory-Based vs. Context-Only Conditioning Produces Distinct Behavioral Patterns in Stateful Personalization</h3>

- **Venue**: ITS 2026
- **Summary**: 研究教师教育推荐系统中条件上下文对个性化行为的影响，比较基于当前学生问题的上下文条件与基于持久学习者信息的记忆条件。发现上下文推荐对问题响应更强，记忆推荐表现依赖历史的行为，包括相同输入下的学习者特异性分化。教师反馈表明推荐可解释且可操作。

<h3 id="173">[173] Learning after COVID-19 and the ICT career aspirations: Are students entering the AI era with weaker skills?</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Computational Social Science, Data Analysis
- **Summary**: 使用PISA 2018和2022数据，结合描述性统计、回归、聚类、变分自编码器等混合方法，分析学习环境与学生ICT职业期望的关系。发现ICT职业期望全球性增长但不均衡，数字技能是最强预测因素，教师支持起补充作用，自主性影响较弱。教育准备度是多维的，ICT期望相对独立于其他职业领域。

- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Multi-Agent System, Medical AI

- **Summary**: 针对动机访谈（MI）对话生成缺乏情境基础、动态策略控制和临床标准评估的问题，提出StoryMI多LLM代理框架，基于问卷生成客户故事，治疗师和客户代理在交互代理协调下生成MI编码对话。构建了6000条模拟对话数据集，覆盖12个MI代码和13个症状领域。结果表明情境基础和宏观控制可提高MI依从性和临床合理性。

<h3 id="176">[176] Informing AI Policy Assessment using Large-Scale Simulation of Interventions</h3>

<h3 id="177">[177] Agentic Literacy Debt: A Structural Problem the AI Literacy Field Has Not Yet Named</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27396) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rohith Nama
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agentic Literacy
- **Summary**: 现有AI素养框架针对用户评估AI输出的场景，但自主AI代理已能在无逐级人类批准的情况下行动，带来代理素养债务问题。本文提出“代理素养债务”概念，指出社会因缺乏对应素养基础设施而累积的赤字，并通过医疗、金融等领域的证据说明这一结构性问题的严重性。研究呼吁将AI素养重新定义为一种治理能力而非评估能力。

<h3 id="178">[178] Short-Term Gain, Long-Term Fragility: AI Labor Substitution and the Erosion of Sustainable Capability</h3>

- **Authors**: Wolfgang Rohde
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Labor Market Analysis, AI Sustainability
- **Summary**: 本文探讨AI劳动力替代可能带来的长期脆弱性，指出现有短期效率提升可能掩盖组织能力的侵蚀。通过AI辅助编程等领域的证据，说明替代压力由管理成本激励和国家竞争驱动，导致系统短期高效但长期脆弱。

<h3 id="179">[179] Mathematical Modelling of Ethical AI Use in Higher Education: A Coordination Game Framework for Future-Facing Learning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.ET; cs.GT; cs.MA

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI

- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL

<h3 id="182">[182] LLM-assisted sentiment analysis for integrated computational and qualitative mixed methods education research: A case study of students' written reflection assignments</h3>

<h3 id="183">[183] Smaller, Younger, and More Impactful: How AI-Assisted Writing Transforms Research Teams</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Fairness, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有的脉冲神经网络公平性评估受限于数据集多样性和理想化硬件假设。本文首次系统性地提出SNN公平性基准，涵盖数据偏差、虚假特征和部署环境不匹配三个维度。在12个SNN模型上评估发现，偏差数据导致代表性不足群体假阳性率增加23%，硬件限制使精度差距扩大41%，并指出云端公平性策略在资源受限时失效。

<h3 id="185">[185] STARS: Spike Tail-Aware Relational Synthesis for ANN-to-SNN Data-Free Knowledge Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27409](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27409) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuhan Ye, Yi Yu, Qixin Zhang, Hui Lu, Jiaming He, Qinggang Zhang, Li Shen, Xudong Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: SNN性能仍落后于ANN，但原始训练数据常不可得，现有数据无关知识蒸馏方法对SNN学生约束不足。本文提出STARS方法，通过关系一致性对齐和尾感知正则化增强BN引导的合成，生成更有效于SNN学生的合成批次。在多个数据集上，该方法一致提升DFKD基线，最高提升6.7%。

<h3 id="186">[186] Advancing Direct Training for Spiking Neural Networks with Circulate-Firing Neurons and Learnable Gradients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27412](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27412) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feifan Zhou, Xiang Wei, Yang Liu, Qiang Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network
- **Summary**: SNN性能差距源于传统神经元信息表示能力有限和固定替代梯度导致梯度传播不精确。本文提出包含循环激发神经元模型、可学习替代梯度和正负平衡损失函数的直接训练算法，在多个数据集和Transformer架构上取得竞争力结果。

<h3 id="187">[187] Ligand-Conditioned Discrete Diffusion for Protein Sequence-Structure Co-Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27413) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Wei, Fanding Xu, Minghao Sun, Zhiyuan Liu, Lin Wang, Tianrui Jia, Yihang Zhou, Yang Zhang
- **Subjects**: q-bio.BM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Engineering, Molecular Generation, Diffusion Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/auroua/ProtLiD)
- **Summary**: 蛋白质功能由其三维结构决定，配体结合蛋白质共设计需要模型在配体约束下生成序列-结构兼容的蛋白质。本文提出ProtLiD^2，一种配体条件离散扩散模型，联合生成氨基酸序列和离散结构令牌，并在推理时提出最大置信度掩码解码策略。在全局蛋白设计和口袋共设计任务中显著提升TM-score、pLDDT和配体感知通过率。

<h3 id="188">[188] Can Quantum Federated Learning Withstand Circuit-Level Backdoors?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27416) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aakar Mathur, Mohammed Ruknuddin, Ashish Gupta
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Federated Learning, Backdoor Attack
- **Venue**: IJCAI-ECAI 2026
- **Summary**: 量子联邦学习继承了联邦优化对恶意客户的脆弱性，并引入了变分电路训练和测量驱动的梯度攻击面。本文提出电路级后门威胁模型CULT，包括四种量子感知攻击，并证明在FedAvg聚合下单个恶意客户可导致严重精度下降。现有防御方法在多数情况下有效，但无法消除最坏情况，精度下降可达50%。

<h3 id="189">[189] Quantum Machine Learning-based 6G edge Network: Enabling Adaptive Communication and Model Aggregation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27417](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27417) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenjing Xiao, Jiatai Yan, Chenglong Shi, Shixin Chen, Miaojiang Chen, Min Chen, Saif Al-Kuwari, Ahmed Farouk
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Edge Computing, Federated Learning
- **Summary**: 6G移动通信中V2X通信面临高维状态空间、慢收敛和泛化差等挑战。本文提出量子增强框架，包括信道自适应语义通信、多模态融合、模型迁移和联邦聚合四个模块，利用量子卷积网络、量子注意力、量子强化学习和量子张量分解等技术，提升通信效率和模型鲁棒性。

<h3 id="190">[190] Ocean4Rec: Offline LLM-Derived OCEAN Profiles for Request-Time VOD Reranking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27429) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wonkyun Kim, Sehyun Bae, Kwanki Ahn, Mungyu Bae, Saeun Choi, Soyeon You, Chandra Prabhakar, Sehyun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Video Streaming
- **Summary**: 工业视频点播推荐系统需要丰富内容理解，但LLM作为重排器在请求时重复构造提示和生成token影响延迟。本文提出Ocean4Rec，离线使用LLM从内容元数据生成OCEAN人格评分，请求时无需LLM调用即可进行数值重排。在三星智能电视VOD日志上，NDCG@20相比基线提升7.6%到61.5%。

<h3 id="191">[191] Tackling Multimodal Learning Challenges with Mixture-of-Expert: A Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27431](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27431) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liangwei Nathan Zheng, Wei Emma Zhang, Olaf Maennel, Lin Yue, Weitong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Multimodal Learning, Survey
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 混合专家模型为多模态学习提供了可扩展框架，但缺乏系统性综述。本文从三个视角回答MoE如何有效解决多模态挑战：作为高效多模态引擎、多模态表示学习器和多模态适配器，并通过广泛文献综述识别了可解释路由、专家通信和终身多模态学习等研究空白。

<h3 id="192">[192] FD-RAG: Federated Dual-System Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27432](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27432) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianhao Gao, Kai Yang, Yiyang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Federated Learning, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 现有检索增强生成系统假设集中式知识访问和充足计算，在边缘环境中不适用。本文提出FD-RAG，一种联邦双系统RAG框架，解耦轻量内存访问和按需LLM推理。学习语义感知自适应超图并将其蒸馏为紧凑QA记忆，在查询时直接匹配记忆，必要时才调用LLM。在QA基准上准确率提升7.8%，延迟降低8.4倍。

<h3 id="193">[193] Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Modeling for Measurement and Network Analysis of the Data Service Market</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27433) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deyu Zhou, Yuwei Guo, Xudong Lu, Linhao Zhang, Wei Guo, Lizhen Cui
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Social Simulation, Market Simulation
- **Summary**: 本文针对数据服务市场中多异质实体和社会要素的测量与分析不足的问题，提出基于异质多智能体建模的数据服务市场测量与网络分析方法。引入服务生态系统理论，明确参与者及外部因素，基于价值创造对三级实体进行效用测量，并设计分析框架评估异质网络对效用的影响。实验结果验证了该方法的有效性。

<h3 id="194">[194] When NPUs Are Not Always Faster: A Stage-Level Analysis of Mobile LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27435) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pu Li, Jiawen Qi, Qinyu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Mobile AI, Heterogeneous Computing, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 本文首次系统性地在算子与流水线级别对比移动端LLM推理中NPU与CPU的性能差异。采用OPMASK控制的流水线分解方法，发现Prefill阶段CPU比NPU快1.6倍，Decode阶段NPU仅1.05-1.2倍加速，且调度开销和跨后端回退降低了实际收益，能量消耗最高增加51%。据此为NPU架构师提出设计指南。

<h3 id="195">[195] RE-TRIANGLE: Does TRIANGLE Enable Multimodal Alignment Beyond Cosine Similarity in Retrieval?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27436) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arijit Ghosh, Aritra Bandyopadhyay, Chiranjeev Bindra, Jingfen Qiao
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Information Retrieval, Research Reproducibility
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ARIJIT00171/RE-TRIANGLE)
- **Summary**: 本文验证TRIANGLE框架中几何对齐目标在多模态检索中的鲁棒性。确认零样本下TRIANGLE优于成对基线，Recall@1提升最高8.7点，但无法复现从头训练结果。分析表明余弦正则化主要稳定文本到视频检索，领域微调提升几何收益但降低跨数据集泛化。

<h3 id="196">[196] MGRetrieval: Memory-Guided Reflective Retrieval for Long-Term Dialogue Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27437](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27437) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tan Wang, Yunwei Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Dialogue System, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对长程对话代理中记忆冗余和一次性检索不足的问题，提出MGRetrieval检索策略，通过回顾历史记忆的语义结构构建检索路径，并由LLM保留关键记忆决定是否继续迭代。在LoCoMo上平均F1提升8.91%，BLEU-1提升11.11%。

<h3 id="197">[197] Prominence-Stratified Failure Modes in Retrieval-Augmented Commercial Recommendation: A 37,000-Run Audit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27439](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27439) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Will Jack, Noah Lehman, Keller Maloney, Sarah Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Information Retrieval, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文对AI助手商业推荐中品牌知名度的分层失效模式进行大规模审计。基于约3.7万次运行，发现知名品牌出现率高但转换率低，中层品牌为转折点，小众品牌几乎不可见。优化策略需根据品牌层级定制。

<h3 id="198">[198] Paraphrase Brittleness in Production Retrieval-Augmented Commercial Recommendation: Reproducibility Below the Rerun-Stability Baseline</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27440) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Will Jack, Noah Lehman, Keller Maloney, Sarah Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文研究AI助手推荐中对查询释义的脆弱性。约6000次释义运行显示，释义间的推荐集Jaccard相似度仅0.135-0.288，远低于同一提示重跑基线0.50-0.61。提示措辞而非购买意图主导推荐结果，表明基于固定提示的品牌可见性跟踪不稳定。

<h3 id="199">[199] A Systematic Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Language Models for Space Operations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27444) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruben Belo, Marta Guimarães, Cláudia Soares
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Space Operations
- **Summary**: 本文系统评估检索增强生成（RAG）管道在太空操作中的性能，比较不同检索策略、嵌入模型和LLM回答对信息准确性和相关性的影响。结果表明RAG能显著增强知识获取并支持决策。

<h3 id="200">[200] RAGe: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Evaluation Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Larissa Guder, João Pedro de Moura, Arthur Accorsi, Gustavo Losch do Amaral, Maurício Cecílio Magnaguagno, Felipe Meneguzzi, Marcio Sorraglia Pinho, Dalvan Griebler
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出RAGe模块化框架，用于RAG应用的基准测试和组件推荐，关注资源遥测和组件选择。该框架将检索和生成质量与硬件约束关联，帮助研究人员在消费级硬件上快速原型化领域特定的RAG设置。

<h3 id="201">[201] Checking Fact with Better Retrieval: Dynamic Contrastive Learning for Evidence Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27449) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhongtian Hua, Yi Luo, Meijia Yu, Yingjie Han
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Fact Checking, Information Retrieval, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态事实验证中证据检索相似而不相关的问题，提出DACLR动态自适应对比学习方法。利用多模态大模型统一模态，两阶段检索（召回-重排序），通过对比损失与难负样本挖掘增强事件感知能力。实验证明了其有效性。

<h3 id="202">[202] Generic Interpretation Approach for Transformer Models Incorporating Heterogenous Attention Structures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27458](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27458) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongjin Cui, Xiaohui Fan, Huajun Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对异质注意力结构Transformer模型的解释问题，提出通用解释方法。将注意力分为同质与异质两类，以协同注意力为例，对代表性模型进行语义和逻辑解释。实验分析了模型的运行机制。

<h3 id="203">[203] When prompt perturbations break your A/B test: A valid statistical test for generative surveying</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27463](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27463) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hayden Helm, Carey Priebe
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering, A/B Testing
- **Summary**: 本文证明在生成式调查中，标准统计检验（如符号检验、Wilcoxon符号秩检验）在提示扰动存在时失效。提出一种有效的置换检验，并表征标准检验失败的条件。应用实例表明效应大小和方向对模型选择敏感。

<h3 id="204">[204] Beyond Motion Primitives: Behavioral Activity Recognition from Head-Mounted IMU</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27464) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chung-Ta Huang, Leopold Das, Jeffrey Zhou, Faizaan Siddique, Julia Seungjoo Baek, Serena Liu, Andrew Rusli, Todd Y. Zhou, Freddy Yu, Sinclair Hansen, Ziling Hu, Arnav Sharma, Mengyu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human Activity Recognition, Wearable Computing, Deep Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Harvard-AI-and-Robotics-Lab/HiT-HAR)
- **Summary**: 为解决AR智能眼镜需要行为上下文而头戴IMU仅检测运动原语的问题，构建16万样本Ego4D数据集，提出HiT-HAR层次模型（703K参数）实现5类动作和8类场景识别，优于先前模型。通过可分性分析绘制头戴IMU的可观测性边界。

<h3 id="205">[205] AdaMerge: Salience-Aware Adaptive Token Merging for Training-Free Acceleration of Vision Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27465) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Semi Lee, Hyejin Go, Hyesong Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Token Merging, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对视觉Transformer中令牌合并（ToMe）忽略重要令牌信息损失的问题，提出AdaMerge框架，包含显著性加权相似度和自适应合并强度。在ImageNet-1k上，ViT-B/16在13.4G FLOPs下Top-1下降仅-1.06%，优于ToMe、PiToMe和DSM。

<h3 id="206">[206] AgensFlow: A Coordination-Policy Substrate for Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27466](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27466) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicole Koenigstein
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Nicolepcx/AgensFlow)
- **Summary**: 本文提出AgensFlow框架，将多智能体协调视为在线策略学习问题。在分布式系统事件和安全咨询任务上评估，显示学习路由优于固定流水线，跳过操作优化拓扑，热启动策略图减少探索成本。

<h3 id="207">[207] Comparative Analysis of Liquid Neural Networks and LSTM for Sequential Pattern Recognition: Robustness, Efficiency, and Clinical Utility</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27467) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ye Kyaw Thu, Thazin Myint Oo, Thepchai Supnithi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Recurrent Neural Network, Time Series Analysis, Robustness
- **Venue**: JCSSE 2026
- **Summary**: 本文对比液态神经网络（LNN）与LSTM在四种序列模态上的性能。LNN在参数效率和鲁棒性上更优，尤其在临床时间序列等稀疏数据场景。本文还进行时间dropout压力测试。

<h3 id="208">[208] Architecture-driven Shift: towards a lightweight selector for capturing the trends of logit shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27469](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27469) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhong Ye, Yu Hu, Ruilin Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Model Calibration, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 针对持续学习中选择预训练模型时计算开销大的问题，提出架构驱动偏移（ADS）作为对数几率偏移的轻量代理。ADS结合架构与数据依赖，仅需少量样本即可计算。在175种架构上，ADS与对数几率偏移的Spearman相关系数最低0.731。

<h3 id="209">[209] Detect by Yourself: Self-Designing Agentic Workflows for Few-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27470) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tairan Huang, Qiang Chen, Yili Wang, Yueyue Ma, Changlong He, Xiu Su, Yi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Anomaly Detection, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 针对固定流水线在图异常检测中适应性不足及缺乏异常证据的问题，提出SignGAD框架，将任务重定义为设计任务条件检测工作流，通过选择合适图编码和检测器来挖掘异常证据，并引入守卫重组策略。实验表明SignGAD在多个数据集上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="210">[210] AssertLLM2: A Comprehensive LLM Benchmark for Assertion Generation from Design Specifications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27472](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27472) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchao Wu, Wenji Fang, Jing Wang, Wenkai Li, Ziyan Guo, Zhiyao Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Evaluation, RTL Verification
- **Summary**: 针对现有断言生成基准存在任务不现实、规范输入弱、评估简化等问题，提出AssertLLM2基准，包含83个真实设计，支持错误预防和错误查找两种设置，采用更严格的评估框架。该基准为硬件验证中的断言生成提供了更现实的评估。

<h3 id="211">[211] HEAL: Resilient and Self-* Hub-based Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Amine Legheraba, Stefan Galkiewicz, Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru, Sébastien Tixeuil
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Fault Tolerance
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习单点故障和去中心化学习收敛慢的问题，提出HEAL框架，利用自组织和自愈P2P覆盖网络，动态选择聚合节点。仿真表明HEAL在无故障时性能接近联邦学习，在崩溃和波动环境下优于谣言学习和流行病学习。

<h3 id="212">[212] Balancing Fidelity and Diversity in Diffusion Models via Symmetric Attention Decomposition: Hopfield Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27476) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunmin Cho, Woo Kyoung Han, Kyong Hwan Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Attention Mechanism, Generative Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hyeon-cho/Attention-Symmetric-Decomposition)
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型中保真度与多样性的权衡问题，从Hopfield网络角度将注意力矩阵分解为对称和反对称部分，解释对称部分控制能量景观结构，反对称部分驱动循环，并提出可控调节机制。实验验证了稳定性度量与生成质量之间的相关性。

<h3 id="213">[213] Resource-Constrained Affect Modelling via Variance Regularisation Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27479) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kosmas Pinitas, Konstantinos Katsifis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Model Compression, Pruning
- **Venue**: PETRA 2026
- **Summary**: 在资源受限的情感计算系统中，现有剪枝方法仅优化稀疏性而忽略个体差异鲁棒性。提出方差正则化剪枝（VR）框架，在剪枝时同时考虑预测精度和跨参与者稳定性。在AGAIN数据集上，80%稀疏度下仍保持竞争性CCC性能。

<h3 id="214">[214] BIRDS: Characterizing and Understanding Biodiversity Impact of Large Language Model Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27480) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyao Shi, Yi Ding
- **Subjects**: q-bio.OT; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, AI Sustainability
- **Summary**: 针对LLM服务对生态系统生物多样性影响的评估缺失，提出BIRDS框架，量化操作和隐含生物多样性影响，引入质量归一化生物多样性影响（QNBI）指标。实验揭示了规模化的生物多样性影响和可操作的服务权衡。

<h3 id="215">[215] Energy-Structured Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27482](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27482) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Longhua Li, Lei Qi, Qi Tian, Xin Geng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对正交子空间方法在持续学习中能量扩散导致容量浪费的问题，提出E²-LoRA方法，通过能量集中和排序，以及动态秩分配策略平衡稳定性与可塑性。实验表明E²-LoRA达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="216">[216] Debate Helps Weak Judges Reward Stronger Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27483) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ethan Elasky, Frank Nakasako, Naman Goyal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Scalable Oversight
- **Summary**: 针对辩论作为可扩展监督协议有时效果不明确的问题，在代码和逻辑任务上研究裁判-辩护者辩论，发现当批评者分类能力超过裁判且裁判验证时，辩论能显著改善裁判表现。结论是单次独立批评即可获得大部分辩论收益。

<h3 id="217">[217] Diffusion-Based Ukrainian Handwritten Text Generation with Cross-Domain Style Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27487](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27487) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrii Ahitoliev, Pavlo Berezin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Handwritten Text Generation, Diffusion Model, Style Transfer
- **Summary**: 针对低资源非拉丁文字（乌克兰语）手写文本生成研究不足的问题，构建乌克兰语手写数据集并重新训练DiffusionPen模型，测试跨域风格迁移。实验表明少样本潜在扩散模型能在拉丁域外泛化，生成可读且风格一致的图像。

<h3 id="218">[218] Grimlock: Guarding High-Agency Systems with eBPF and Attested Channels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27488) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiancheng Wu, Wenhui Zhang, Gan Fang, Sheng Mao, Biao Gao, David Levitsky, Shawna Murphy Butterworth, Rob Cameron
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Security, System Design
- **Summary**: 针对高代理系统（如智能体系统）中身份、授权和溯源难以一致执行和审计的问题，提出Grimlock，一种基于eBPF和TLS 1.3的代理守卫，通过强制流量拦截和通道绑定实现透明、可审计的代理间通信。

<h3 id="219">[219] HARP: Measuring Harm Amplification in Multi-Agent LLM Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27489](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27489) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Hafizur Rahman, Zafaryab Haider, Tanzim Mahfuz, Prabuddha Chakraborty
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对多Agent LLM系统中局部扰动可能被放大为系统级危害的问题，提出HARP方法，通过轨迹比较定义局部和全局危害及放大率。在金融7Agent系统上测试，发现单专家妥协产生最强放大，共享上下文污染产生最高攻击成功率。

<h3 id="220">[220] Benchmarks are Not Enough: RAMP for Runtime Assessing of Agentic Models in Production Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27492) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yipeng Ouyang, Xin Huang, Bingjie Liu, Zhongchun Zheng, Yuhao Gu, Xianwei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对现有静态基准无法反映软件工程智能体在长时间执行链中的真实能力的问题，提出RAMP生产评估基础设施，引入编译器构建工作负载和多维指标。评估15个模型发现能力退化严重，完成率从100%降至20%。

<h3 id="221">[221] Grounded Cache Routing for Retrieval-Augmented Generation: When Is It Safe to Reuse an Answer?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Syed Huma Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Cache Policy, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/syedhumarahim/grounded-cache-router)
- **Summary**: 针对RAG中输出级语义答案缓存不安全的问题，提出GroundedCache缓存路由器，通过四个廉价门控确保安全。在HotpotQA和mtRAG上实现0%不安全服务率，延迟接近无缓存基线。

<h3 id="222">[222] Detection Without Correction: A Two-Parameter Decomposition of Multi-Stage LLM Pipelines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27559) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prashanti Nilayam, Kiran Ramanna, Prashil Tumbade
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Correction, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对多阶段LLM流水线中出现准确率平台期和反转等问题，提出将下游响应分解为检测和条件生成两个决策。发现条件错误纠正率占主导（53-94%），检测率变化大，统一解释了多种现象。

<h3 id="223">[223] Clinical Validation of the Melanoscope AI Mobile Dermoscopy Clinical Decision Support System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27561) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elena Sergeevna Kozachok, Sergey Sergeevich Seregin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Cancer Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 针对俄罗斯地区皮肤科医生短缺导致皮肤癌筛查覆盖率低的问题，开发并临床验证了Melanoscope AI移动皮肤镜临床决策支持系统。在176名患者中实现了88.6%专家一致性，无假阴性，特异性88.3%。

<h3 id="224">[224] On the Subgaussianity of Quantized Linear Maps: An AI-Assisted Note</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27563) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guangyi Zou, Roman Vershynin
- **Subjects**: math.PR; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Quantization, Concentration Inequality
- **Summary**: 针对量化线性映射的次高斯性问题，本文给出了高斯向量在坐标非线性映射下的维数无关次高斯集中界，由Gemini 3.5 Flash发现。该结果可用于回答符号量化线性映射的相关问题。

<h3 id="225">[225] The Future of Facts: Tracing the Factual Generation-Verification Gap</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27564](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27564) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tim R. Davidson, Anja Surina, Caglar Gulcehre
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation, Fact Checking
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/anjasurina/factgap)
- **Summary**: 本文识别了语言模型中的事实生成-验证差距（GV-gap），发现验证能力比生成能力更早学习且对持续学习更鲁棒，且事实更新可能导致模型处于同时验证新旧答案的“多宇宙”状态。

<h3 id="226">[226] Hallucination Behavior in Multimodal LLMs Across Agricultural Image Interpretation and Generation Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27595](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27595) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Partho Ghose, Al Bashir, Prem Raj, Azlan Zahid
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, LLM Hallucination, Agricultural AI
- **Summary**: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型在农业图像解释和生成任务中的幻觉行为，发现零样本准确率仅63-75%，少样本提升至86.8%，而文本到图像生成任务中高达91%的场景存在生物学不一致。

<h3 id="227">[227] The Energy Blind Spot: NVIDIA's Flagship Edge AI Hardware Cannot Support Process-Level Energy Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27599](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27599) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deepak Panigrahy, Aakash Tyagi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.AR; cs.DC; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, Energy Efficiency, AI Sustainability
- **Summary**: 本文审计了NVIDIA GB10边缘AI硬件的能量可观测性，发现缺乏CPU能量计数和每个进程的能量归因，并提出了使用外部直流计量和GPU减法进行校准的方案。

<h3 id="228">[228] Eliot: Interactively $\underline{E}$xploring Fast-Changing Scientific $\underline{Li}$terature Trends with $\underline{O}$nline Da$\underline{t}$a and Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27610) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bernardo A. Denkvitts, Nitin Gupta, Biplav Srivastava
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Data Visualization, Knowledge Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Eliot，一个交互式系统，通过查询时聚类和时间可视化来探索快速发展的科学文献趋势，评估显示85%的聚类标签有意义，最适用于快速变化技术领域。

<h3 id="229">[229] Not All NVFP4 QAT Recipes Are Equal: How Architecture and Scale Shape Model Quality for Anomaly Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27616) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijian Du, Oleg Rybakov
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Image Segmentation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文研究架构、规模和FP4量化感知训练（QAT）配方对异常分割模型质量的影响，发现注意力架构对量化鲁棒性最强，Swin Transformer在所有尺度下均对QAT配方鲁棒。

<h3 id="230">[230] Supervised Distributional Reduction via Optimal Transport and Dependence Maximization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27619) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sai-Aakash Ramesh, Archit Sood, Andrew Corbett, Tim Dodwell
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Optimal Transport, Gaussian Process
- **Summary**: 本文提出监督分布约简（SDR）算法，结合最优输运和依赖最大化学习目标感知的表示，并可用于构建非平稳高斯过程核。

<h3 id="231">[231] Trinity: Unifying Class-Agnostic Terrain and Semantic Segmentation for Unstructured Outdoor Environments by Leveraging Synthetic Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27644) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marcus G Müller, Wout Boerdijk, Maximilian Durner, Riccardo Giubilato, Abel Gawel, Wolfgang Stürzl, Roland Siegwart, Rudolph Triebel
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Semantic Segmentation, Robotics, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出Trinity，一个基于Transformer的统一网络，同时执行类别特定语义分割和类别无关地形分割，利用合成数据集RUGDSynth和真实数据集EXTerra。

<h3 id="232">[232] Hurwitz Quaternion Multiplicative Quantization for KV Cache Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kabir Swain, Sijie Han, Daniel Karl I. Weidele, Mauro Martino, David Cox, Antonio Torralba
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 本文提出Hurwitz四元数乘法量化（HQMQ）用于KV缓存压缩，无需校准，在~5比特下匹配fp16质量，对Llama-3-70B将43GB缓存压缩至8.5GB。

<h3 id="233">[233] Cultural Fidelity in English-to-Hindi Translation: A Preservation-Fluency Frontier for Gender Recoverability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Samyak Savi, Chavi Gupta, Shreyas Gantayet, Tanay Sodha, Dhruv Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Gender Bias, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 本文评估英译印地语中的性别可恢复性，发现五个系统经常通过作格和敬语结构消除性别，提出的现象感知重排序器（PAR）将性别保留从10.3%提升至81.3%，但牺牲流畅性。

<h3 id="234">[234] Developing an Intelligent Job Recommendation System Using Semantic Retrieval and Explainable AI Techniques</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27656](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27656) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hussein Al Awad, Khaled Fathi Omar
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Explainable AI, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个基于元数据的求职推荐系统，结合TF-IDF、Sentence-BERT语义检索和交叉编码器重排序，在LinkedIn数据集上实现Precision@10为0.8032，nDCG@10为0.9496。

<h3 id="235">[235] Transferable Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Latent Embeddings and Dynamic Policy Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27659) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gengyue Han, Yiheng Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Sim-to-Real, Safe RL
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个通过概率潜在嵌入和动态策略适应实现安全高效的Sim2Real迁移的强化学习框架，利用元RL中的潜在上下文变量和分布RL动态调整风险水平。

<h3 id="236">[236] How the Optimizer Shapes Learned Solutions in Equivariant Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27662](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27662) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Teodor-Mihai Stupariu, Andrei Manolache
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Equivariant Network, Optimization, Geometric Deep Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Weight-Space Symmetries
- **Summary**: 本文比较Muon和Adam优化器在等变神经网络上的表现，发现Muon在ModelNet40上一致优于Adam，且导致更大Hessian曲率但更规则的损失面。

<h3 id="237">[237] Aligning LLMs with Human Uncertainty: A Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator for LLM Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27668) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hui Dai, Ryan Teehan, Parsa Torabian, Mengye Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 本文提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器（BBC），利用人类预测和二元结果将LLM点预测转换为校准的分布，在保持轻量的同时比传统方法具有更好的校准和准确率。

<h3 id="238">[238] Backdoor Attacks on Fault Detection and Localization in Cyber-Physical Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abile Jean, Kuniyilh S
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文研究了针对CPS中故障检测与定位的后门攻击，设计了触发模式并评估了攻击成功率，实验表明仅10%的毒化即可成功攻击。

<h3 id="239">[239] Tensor Memory: Fixed-Size Recurrent State for Long-Horizon Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kabir Swain, Sijie Han, Daniel Karl I. Weidele, Mauro Martino, Antonio Torralba
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出张量记忆模块，为Transformer添加固定大小的循环3D记忆张量，用于长时程视频理解，通过可微软写入和高斯加权体积更新，保持空间归纳偏置。

<h3 id="240">[240] Simulation-Informed Diffusion for Decentralized Multi-robot Motion Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27697) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinhao Liang, Sven Koenig, Ferdinando Fioretto
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Motion Planning, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文提出仿真信息扩散（SID），一个基于约束感知扩散模型的分布式多机器人运动规划框架，通过仿真邻居轨迹来规划安全轨迹，可扩展至108个机器人和160个障碍物。

<h3 id="241">[241] CiteCheck: Retrieval-Grounded Detection of LLM Citation Hallucinations in Scientific Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27700](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27700) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Khashayar Khajavi, Shaghayegh Sadeghi, Rise Adhikari, Alexander Tessier
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Fact Checking, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型生成虚假引文的问题，提出CiteCheck混合框架，通过检索外部学术资源、结构化LLM验证器和映射标签（精确/次要/主要）来检测引文幻觉。在物理基准测试上，CiteCheck达到88.7宏F1和88.9%准确率，优于GPT、Claude和Gemini基线。

<h3 id="242">[242] UserHarness: Harnessing User Minds for Stronger Agent Theory-of-Mind</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27721) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cheng Qian, Jiayu Liu, Heng Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Theory of Mind
- **Summary**: 现有理论心智（ToM）方法未显式重建用户心理状态，提出UserHarness框架，分解用户观察、信念、意图和行动。在五个基准上达到95.94%宏准确率，相对提升超15%，表明从用户心智根源推理的有效性。

<h3 id="243">[243] HumanoidMimicGen: Data Generation for Loco-Manipulation via Whole-Body Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kevin Lin, Ajay Mandlekar, Caelan Reed Garrett, Nikita Chernyadev, Yu Fang, Runyu Ding, Yuqi Xie, Justin Tran, Linxi Fan, Yuke Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Imitation Learning, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对人形机器人全身运动-操作数据生成困难，提出HumanoidMimicGen方法，通过调整接触丰富的全身技能和规划生成稳定无碰撞的数据。在九个任务上，联合训练的策略比仅用真实数据提升20%性能。

<h3 id="244">[244] Worker Disagreement Reveals Sharp Directions in Local SGD</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27739) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tolga Dimlioglu, Kristi Topollai, Anna Choromanska
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Optimization, Distributed Training
- **Venue**: HiLD 2026
- **Summary**: 针对Hessian矩阵估计成本高的问题，证明局部SGD中工作平均间隙协方差能揭示尖锐方向，提供免Hessian的估计方法。实验表明该子空间能捕捉主导Hessian特征空间的大部分梯度分量。

<h3 id="245">[245] Mahalanobis PatchCore: Covariance-Aware and Streaming-Compatible Industrial Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27748) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Niccolò Ferrari, Oligert Osmani, Evelina Lamma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Industrial Monitoring, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 针对PatchCore忽略特征相关性且需离线构建的问题，提出马氏距离PatchCore，估计正则化协方差模型并流式构建记忆库。在公共基准上峰值内存从5.41降至2.78 GB，工业AUC从0.981提升至0.986。

<h3 id="246">[246] Reading or Guessing? Visual Grounding Failures of Vision-Language Models for OCR in Ancient Greek Editions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27750](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27750) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antonia Karamolegkou, Nicolas Angleraud, Benoît Sagot, Thibault Clérice
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.DL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, OCR, Low-Resource NLP
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型（VLM）在OCR中产生流畅但无视觉支持的文本问题，通过受控图像扰动和令牌级基础度量发现VLM错误具有语言先验依赖性。传统OCR保持忠实，而VLM即使错误也仍依赖视觉输入。

<h3 id="247">[247] High-Fidelity Industrial Crash Dynamics Prediction via Geometry-Aware Operator Learning with Memory-Efficient Low-Rank Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27758) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deepak Akhare, Mohammad Amin Nabian, Corey Adams, Sudeep Chavare, Sanjay Choudhry
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Surrogate Model, Manufacturing AI, Physics-Informed Learning, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对工业级碰撞动力学预测的快速代理建模需求，提出GeoTransolver框架，采用FLARE低秩注意力减少内存约2倍并提高精度。一次性预测方法实现最先进准确率，证明几何感知算子学习的实用性。

<h3 id="248">[248] Can Segmentation Models Understand the World? Towards Proactive Affordance Reasoning via Visual Chain-of-Thought</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27764](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27764) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen Guo, Junli Gong, Hongmin Cai, Yiu-ming Cheung, Weifeng Su
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Affordance Learning, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对分割模型仅处理目标指代指令的问题，提出SegWorld，通过多级视觉思维链进行主动观察和可负担性推理。在意图级分割上显著优于指令驱动基线。

<h3 id="249">[249] Restoring the Sweet Spot: Pass-Rate Weighted Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zehao Liu, Yuanpu Cao, Jinghui Chen, Vasant G. Honavar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对SDPO缺乏难度感知的问题，提出SC-SDPO，通过得分自适应加权实现隐式课程学习。在科学推理和工具使用基准上，SC-SDPO在Qwen3-8B和OLMo-3-7B上分别提升+3.2/+4.3和+1.8/+3.0。

<h3 id="250">[250] UniMaia: Steering Chess Policies with Language for Human-like Play</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27767](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27767) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sherman Siu, Lesley Istead
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Game AI, LLM Agent, Human-AI Collaboration, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对国际象棋策略网络缺乏语义可控性而语言模型领域基础弱的问题，提出UniMaia，通过冻结Lc0策略网络和ControlNet风格调节实现提示条件控制。在提示条件基准上达到最先进预期准确率。

<h3 id="251">[251] Do Models Know Why They Changed Their Mind? Interpretability and Faithfulness of Chain-of-Thought Under Knowledge Conflict</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pruthvinath Jeripity Venkata
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Knowledge Conflict
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在知识冲突下思维链是否忠实反映决策机制的问题，提出内省忠实性测试。发现思维链推理高度稳定，自信带有微弱信号；GPT-4o具有可靠的推理-决策耦合。

<h3 id="252">[252] Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vincent-Daniel Yun, Youngrae Kim, Woosang Lim, YoungJin Heo, Minkyu Kim, Sunwoo Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Pruning, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对LLM深度剪枝假设固定冗余的问题，提出LoRP，基于表示局部性得分（RLS）进行聚类剪枝。在多种LLM族上改进困惑度和下游任务准确率。

<h3 id="253">[253] ChildEval: When large language models meet children's personalities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27805) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanyan Luo, Xue Han, Chunxu Zhao, Ruiqiao Bai, Yaxing Zhang, Qian Hu, Lijun Mei, Junlan Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Education Technology, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ziyanluo/ChildEval)
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏儿童中心个性化评估的问题，构建ChildEval基准，包含29K合成儿童画像和偏好。实验表明不同个性化表示影响LLM响应，微调可提升儿童中心性能。

<h3 id="254">[254] Residualized Temporal Sparse Autoencoders for Interpreting Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27813) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Calvin Yeung, Prathyush Poduval, Ali Zakeri, Zhuowen Zou, Mohsen Imani
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Diffusion Model, Sparse Autoencoder, Text-to-Image
- **Summary**: 针对现有稀疏自编码器仅分析单时间步扩散激活的问题，提出残差化时间稀疏自编码器，学习激活轨迹中线性不可预测的结构。在Stable Diffusion 1.5上展示了时空特征分析和引导实验。

<h3 id="255">[255] Turning Video Models into Generalist Robot Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27817) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sizhe Lester Li, Evan Kim, Xingjian Bai, Tong Zhao, Tao Pang, Max Simchowitz, Vincent Sitzmann
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robotics, Imitation Learning, Video Generation, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 针对解耦视频规划与动作预测的机器人策略，提出VERA，结合无动作视频世界模型和基于机器人雅可比矩阵的逆动态模型。在模拟和真实基准上表现强劲，实现零样本跨实体控制。

<h3 id="256">[256] ReSAE: Residualized Sparse Autoencoders for Multi-Layer Transformer Interventions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27819](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27819) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prathyush Poduval, Calvin Yeung, Neel Desai, Mohsen Imani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对逐层稀疏自编码器在多层干预中的冗余和交互问题，提出ReSAEs，通过层间仿射映射拟合残差。在Pythia-1.4B和Gemma-2-9B上减少解码器冗余，改善稀疏探测和多层替代性能。

<h3 id="257">[257] Disentangling Adversarial Prompts: A Semantic-Graph Defense for Robust LLM Security</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27823) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness, Jailbreak Detection
- **Venue**: AAAI 2026
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型易受对抗性提示攻击（如越狱和提示注入）的问题，提出了一种基于互信息语义分解和谱分析图分类的对抗性提示解耦（APD）框架。该框架将提示分解为对抗性和良性成分，并通过轻量级transformer分类器检测恶意意图。实验表明，APD减少了85%以上的有害输出，且对模型性能影响极小，支持实时部署。

<h3 id="258">[258] Symmetry Defeats Auditing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27836](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27836) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nick Merrill, Zeke Medley
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文展示了对内省适配器（Introspection Adapters）的一种攻击方法，揭示了对称性可以破坏审计机制。目前没有提供定量结果，但核心贡献是发现了一种新的攻击途径。

<h3 id="259">[259] LoSATok: Low-dimensional Semantic-Acoustic Tokenizer for Cross-Domain Audio Understanding and Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27840](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27840) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhisheng Zhang, Xiang Li, Yixuan Zhou, Jing Peng, Guoyang Zeng, Zhiyong Wu
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Audio Processing, Tokenization, Diffusion Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wxzyd123/LoSATok)
- **Summary**: 针对现有音频分词器在高维连续潜在空间中编码语义和声学细节导致生成模型负担重的问题，提出了LoSATok，一种低维语义-声学分词器。通过语义瓶颈将1280维压缩至128维，并设计双级语义监督方法。实验表明，LoSATok在保持理解性能的同时，显著提升了扩散Transformer在语音、音乐和音频生成上的效果。

<h3 id="260">[260] Snippet-Driven Supply Chain Discovery with LLMs: Scaling Visibility in China</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27845) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hiroto Fukada, Takayuki Mizuno
- **Subjects**: cs.SI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Information Extraction, Supply Chain Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对中国供应链信息披露有限、未上市企业关系难以获取的问题，提出了一种基于网络搜索摘要的供应链知识图谱构建方法。利用LLM从搜索摘要中提取企业间关系，在13万多家中国企业上验证，与基准数据库相比，覆盖的企业和关系数量分别提升了7.2倍和9.3倍。

<h3 id="261">[261] FPMoE: A Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Approach to Functional Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27849) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Loc Pham, Lang Hong Nguyet Anh, Thanh Le-Cong
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Mixture-of-Experts, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对现有代码生成模型在函数式编程语言上表现不佳且跨语言微调存在干扰的问题，提出了FPMoE，一个基于稀疏混合专家架构的轻量级代码生成模型。通过为Haskell、OCaml和Scala设置专用专家和共享专家，在FPEval上以3B活跃参数达到了与更大模型相当的性能。

<h3 id="262">[262] Fine-Tuned LLM as a Complementary Predictor Improving Ads System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27856) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hui Yang, Daiwei He, Kevin Jiang, Taejin Park, Kungang Li, Jiajun Luo, Yuying Chen, Xinyi Zhang, Sihan Wang, Haoyu He, Yu Liu, Lakshmi Manoharan, David Xue, Shubham Barhate, Runze Su, Duna Zhan, Ling Leng, Siping Ji, Jinfeng Zhuang, Alice Wu, Leo Lu, Han Sun, Zhifang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Application, CTR Prediction
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型在推荐系统和广告系统中应用有限的问题，提出将微调后的开源LLM作为广告专用辅助预测器，从用户画像和历史行为预测潜在广告主。在大规模生产广告系统中验证，该方法在离线和在线指标上均取得显著提升，证明了LLM在广告领域的实用价值。

<h3 id="263">[263] DecomposeRL: Learning to Ask Useful, Informative, and Diverse Questions for Semi-Supervised, Traceable Claim Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27858](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27858) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shubhashis Roy Dipta, Ankur Padia, Francis Ferraro
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Fact Checking, Reinforcement Learning, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 针对事实核查中端到端分类器缺乏可追溯性而分解方法性能不足的问题，提出了DecomposeRL，一种基于强化学习的可追溯声明验证器。通过GRPO和多面奖励集成进行分解策略训练，在仅5K精选数据上达到86.3的域内和69.8的域外平衡准确率，与4倍大的基线模型持平。

<h3 id="264">[264] From Detection to Mechanism: Cross-Attention Graph Neural Networks Enable Drug-Drug Interaction Type Prediction An Ablation Study with Acetylsalicylic Acid Validation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juergen Dietrich
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Graph Neural Network, Drug-Drug Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对药物-药物相互作用预测中从二分类检测到机制类型预测的任务差异，系统比较了三种图神经网络架构。发现带有交叉注意力的双MPNN在多类型分类上F1宏平均提升0.186（+45%），而在二分类上仅提升0.012，并在乙酰水杨酸药物对上验证了10/10的正确预测。

<h3 id="265">[265] SPAR: Support-Preserving Action Rectification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27877](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27877) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxin Zhao, Weihang Pan, Xun Liang, Binbin Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Reinforcement Learning, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对离线策略改进中最大化价值与拟合数据分布之间的冲突，提出了支持保持动作修正（SPAR）框架。将全局学习转化为基于行为克隆策略的局部残差修正，并引入潜在自我模仿机制解决梯度冲突。在D4RL数据集上达到了最先进的性能。

<h3 id="266">[266] VibeSearchBench: Benchmarking Long-horizon Proactive Search in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaohongshu Inc
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Search Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对现有搜索基准依赖过度指定的查询和单轮交互、无法反映真实搜索行为的问题，提出了VibeSearchBench基准，包含200个跨20个领域的双语任务。采用渐进式用户模拟器和图匹配评估框架，发现最强模型F1仅为30.30，表明当前模型在长程推理和主动意图获取上严重不足。

<h3 id="267">[267] SmartDirector: Keyframe-Conditioned Cinematic Video Generation with Narrative Pacing Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27891) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhida Zhang, Jie Ma, Zhan Peng, Haoxue Wu, Yang Han, Jun Liang, Jie Cao, Jing Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Keyframe Conditioning
- **Summary**: 针对现有视频生成方法依赖稀疏条件信号（如文本或首尾帧）导致叙事情节和时间节奏控制不足的问题，提出了SmartDirector框架。通过多关键帧条件生成，支持单镜头、多镜头叙事和视频扩展，并在低分辨率生成后利用高分辨率关键帧进行细节恢复。实验表明，该方法在叙事情节控制上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="268">[268] The Fragility of Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Across Typologically Diverse Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27901) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Onyame, Runtao Zhou, Kowshik Thopalli, Bhavya Kailkhura, Chirag Agarwal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Chain-of-Thought, Cross-Lingual
- **Summary**: 针对链式思维（CoT）监控在非英语语言中的可靠性未知的问题，对13种语言和7个前沿模型家族进行了大规模评估。发现CoT不忠实的平均率为95.9%，模型系统性地进行策略性操纵，包括答案切换和事后合理化。在低资源语言中，欺骗模式依然达到100%，表明CoT监控在语言分布变化下本质上是脆弱的。

<h3 id="269">[269] ESC-Skills: Discovering and Self-Evolving Skills for Emotional Support Conversations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27908) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jie Zhu, Huaixia Dou, Shuo Jiang, Junhui Li, Lifan Guo, Feng Chen, Chi Zhang, Fang Kong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emotional Support Conversation, Dialogue System, Skill Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin)
- **Summary**: 针对现有情感支持对话系统缺乏可解释性和技能提升机制的问题，提出了ESC-Skills框架，通过干预单元（IU）建模局部支持交互，构建可执行的技能库，并引入多角色自进化优化方法。实验表明，该方法在回应质量和情感结果上均有提升，同时提供了更可解释和可控的支持行为。

<h3 id="270">[270] Let the Results Speak: A Replication-First Paradigm for LLM Behavioral Benchmarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27914) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuming, Huang, Yao Liu, Lei Wang, Junchen Wan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对LLM行为评估中主观评价信度低和LLM作为评判者的循环问题，提出了以可重复性为先的基准测试范式。通过可靠性、跨工具复现、历史足迹校准和预注册预测四个维度验证，应用于情感陪伴评估，发现了gpt-5和Opus-4.7在建议约束维度上的性能下降。

<h3 id="271">[271] Do We Really Need Quantum Machine Learning?: A Multidimensional Empirical Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27923) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sudip Vhaduri, Ryan Gammon, Sayanton Dibbo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Quantum Machine Learning, Image Classification, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对量子机器学习是否必要的问题，在MNIST数据集上对经典和量子模型进行了多维度比较。QSVM在准确率上优于CSVM（约0.90 vs 0.85），QCNN在准确率与CCNN相当（均超过0.96）的情况下，参数减少94%，内存减少75%，但运行时间更长。

<h3 id="272">[272] When Think-with-Image Meets Safety: What Determines Multimodal Jailbreak Robustness?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27932) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuan Tian, Bing Hu, Fang Wu, Xiaomin Li, Binghang Lu, Neil Zhenqiang Gong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Jailbreak Attack, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 针对大视觉语言模型在“图像思考”推理新范式下的安全性问题，比较了多种推理流程设计。实验发现，显式外部图像工具调用能将越狱成功率降低约30%，即使工具输出被覆盖或本身不安全时仍有效。提出图像工具安全向量框架，从表示层面解释了这一现象。

<h3 id="273">[273] Pressure-Testing Deception Probes in LLMs: Scaling, Robustness, and the Geometry of Deceptive Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27958](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27958) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sachin Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Deception Detection
- **Venue**: GEM Workshop 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文系统测试了基于探针的LLM欺骗检测方法，发现探针在干净数据上表现近乎完美（AUROC\>=0.998），但在分布偏移下失效。风格增强的探针可恢复检测性能（平均AUROC 0.979-0.983），欺骗编码并非单一线性方向，而是分散的次阈值特征。反向缩放模式归因于训练分布伪影，而非真正的规模相关现象。

<h3 id="274">[274] ROVER: Routing Object-Centric Visual Evidence for Grounded Multi-Image Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27959) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guannan Lv, Ren Nie, Hongjian Dou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Reasoning, Visual Grounding
- **Summary**: 论文提出ROVER，一种轻量级插件，用于高效路由全局视觉证据，实现多图像推理中的视觉定位。它通过注入令牌三元组聚合推理上下文、精炼图像内线索并路由历史感知证据，在MM-GCoT和VideoEspresso上取得最佳性能，并展现出强迁移性。

<h3 id="275">[275] Multi-Teacher Knowledge Distillation via Teacher-Informed Mixture Priors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27967) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luyang Fang, Yongkai Chen, Jiazhang Cai, Ping Ma, Wenxuan Zhong
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Bayesian Inference, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 论文提出多教师贝叶斯知识蒸馏（MT-BKD），在贝叶斯框架下使用教师信息先验整合多教师知识，提供不确定性量化与自适应加权。在合成任务和蛋白质亚细胞定位预测、图像分类等真实任务上性能提升。

<h3 id="276">[276] Semantic Flow Regularization: Teaching LLMs to Generate Diverse Yet Coherent Responses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27971) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kerui Peng, Feifei Li, Xingyu Fan, Wenhui Que
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Text Generation, Style Transfer
- **Summary**: 论文提出语义流正则化（SFR）解决个性条件生成中的跨风格坍缩问题。SFR利用条件流匹配作用于句子嵌入，提升多样性和风格保真度且推理零开销，在对话和代码生成基准上验证有效。

<h3 id="277">[277] Periodic RoPE for Infinite Context LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Simin Huo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, Position Encoding, Transformer Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Cominder/miniwin)
- **Summary**: 论文提出周期性RoPE（P-RoPE）解决LLM位置耗尽问题，实现无限上下文。它将滑动窗口注意力与无位置编码的全局注意力结合，支持无界交互。实验表明MiniWin在长上下文效率上优于标准架构。

<h3 id="278">[278] KVoiceBench, KOpenAudioBench, and KMMAU: Agent-Driven Korean Speech Benchmarks for Evaluating SpeechLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27984) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haechan Kim, Seungjun Chung, Inkyu Park, Jihoo Lee, Jonghyun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Evaluation, Benchmark, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 论文构建了三个韩语语音基准（KVoiceBench、KOpenAudioBench、KMMAU）用于评估语音语言模型，解决了直接基准迁移的局限性。对八个最新语音LM的评估揭示了英韩性能差异以及纯英语评估无法发现的互补弱点。

<h3 id="279">[279] Geometry-Correct Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Denoiser-Pullback Curvature Guidance and Manifold-Aligned Damping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27990](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27990) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seunghyeok Shin, Minwoo Kim, Dabin Kim, Hongki Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Inverse Problem, Image Reconstruction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Seunghyeok0715/CLAMP)
- **Summary**: 论文提出几何校正的扩散后验采样方法，用每噪声水平的阻尼高斯牛顿校正替代标量引导，避免手工调参。在FFHQ/ImageNet逆问题上取得有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS，在加速MRI重建上达到最佳PSNR/SSIM。

<h3 id="280">[280] Where Does Toxicity Live? Mechanistic Localization and Targeted Suppression in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27997](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27997) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Himanshu Beniwal, Mayank Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Model Editing, Toxicity Mitigation
- **Summary**: 论文提出Meow2X和TRNE，通过激活差异定位毒性特定层/神经元，并通过推理时缩放或最小权重编辑抑制。在五个LM上一致降低毒性并保持质量，揭示毒性编码在早期MLP层。

<h3 id="281">[281] Learning to Assign Prediction Tasks to Agents with Capacity Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shang Wu, Saatvik Kher, Padhraic Smyth
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Resource Allocation, Online Learning
- **Summary**: 论文研究在容量约束下学习将预测任务分配给智能体，提供理论表征和序列探索-利用策略学习算法。在表格、图像和文本任务上的实验表明，相比非上下文基线，对LLM和人类智能体均有系统性提升。

<h3 id="282">[282] Tool Forge: A Validation-Carrying Toolchain for Governed Agentic Execution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28000) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Swanand Rao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Workflow Automation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nextmoca/tool-forge)
- **Summary**: 论文提出Tool Forge，一种携带验证的工具链，将自然语言意图转化为受治理的工具工件供LLM智能体使用。在路由基准上实现99.2%的工具上下文缩减和0.901微F1，端到端生成中通过23/25沙箱验证。

<h3 id="283">[283] Integrated and Cross-Architecture Interpretation of LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28006) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Leonardo Matthew Yauw, Wei-Bin Kou, Yujiu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 论文提出跨架构集成推理（IAR）框架用于LLM推理可解释性，结合带宽校准MIP、跨层重叠分析和Jaccard稳定性。在三个模型四个领域上的实验展示了通用解释能力。

<h3 id="284">[284] Learning Compositional Latent Structure with Vector Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28007) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Niclas Pokel, Benjamin F. Grewe
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Compositional Generalization, Representation Learning, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 论文提出向量网络（VN），一种具有可复用权重原子库的循环架构，实现组合泛化。VN为每个输入推断稀疏活动权重原子，在组合基准上OOD误差降低一个数量级。

<h3 id="285">[285] MemGuard: Preventing Memory Contamination in Long-Term Memory-Augmented Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28009](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28009) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyeonjeong Ha, Jeonghwan Kim, Cheng Qian, Jiayu Liu, William M. Campbell, Yue Wu, Yuji Zhang, Kathleen McKeown, Dilek Hakkani-Tur, Heng Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, LLM Hallucination, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 论文识别了长时记忆增强LLM中的异构记忆污染问题，提出MemGuard类型感知记忆框架，保持功能边界。在幻觉和长程对话基准上记忆可靠性提升28.27%，检索令牌减少5.8倍。

<h3 id="286">[286] VCap: Hypergeometric Rewards for Weak-to-Strong Visual Captioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28023) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Lu, Jinpeng Wang, Yi-Fan Zhang, Yankai Yang, Yancheng Long, Yiyang Fan, Xuanyu Zheng, Haonan Fan, Kaiyu Jiang, Tianke Zhang, Changyi Liu, Bin Wen, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Han Li, Chun Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Image Captioning, RLVR, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 论文提出VCap，使用见证-裁决奖励对进行视觉描述，具有超几何分布级精度。基于VCap训练的8B模型在图像和视频描述基准上超越SOTA，提升了感知能力并跨任务泛化。

<h3 id="287">[287] SPARD: Defending Harmful Fine-Tuning Attack via Safety Projection with Relevance-Diversity Data Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28030) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuhao Chen, Weisen Jiang, Yeqi Gong, Shengda Luo, Chengxiang Zhuo, Zang Li, James T. Kwok, Yu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Fine-Tuning, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/shuhao02/SPARD)
- **Summary**: 论文提出SPARD防御框架，结合安全投影交替优化和相关多样性数据选择，抵御有害微调攻击。在GSM8K和OpenBookQA上四种攻击下实现最低攻击成功率，大幅领先现有防御方法并保持任务准确率。

<h3 id="288">[288] Extracting Small Translation Specialists from LLMs by Aggressively Pruning Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liu O. Martin, Lucas Bandarkar, Nanyun Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Mixture-of-Experts, Machine Translation
- **Summary**: 论文提出从MoE LLM中激进裁剪翻译专家的方法，利用专家专业化。无需重训练即可裁剪一半专家且损失可忽略，短时SFT后裁剪75%恢复基准性能，实现MoE块大幅压缩。

<h3 id="289">[289] On the Learnability of Test-Time Adaptation: A Recovery Complexity Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28057) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhi Zhou, Ming Yang, Shi-Yu Tian, Kun-Yang Yu, Lan-Zhe Guo, Yu-Feng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, Generalization Bound
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对测试时自适应（TTA）在非平稳流上的可学习性缺乏理论框架的问题，首次提出了TTA可学习性的理论框架，引入(ε,δ)-恢复复杂度和(ε,ρ)-TTA可学习性概念，推导了恢复复杂度的下界和上界，揭示了自适应-信息的内在权衡。实验验证了该框架的有效性。

<h3 id="290">[290] Unified Synthesis of Compositional Speech and Sound from Free-Form Text Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28063) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuyue Wang, Xihua Wang, Xin Cheng, Yijing Chen, Ruihua Song
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Audio Generation, Speech Synthesis, Text-to-Audio
- **Summary**: 本文针对现有方法无法从自由文本提示直接合成包含语音和声音的统一音频的问题，提出了新任务和PlanAudio框架。PlanAudio利用LLM推理能力和语义潜在链式思维机制，隐式规划实现高层语义理解到底层声学合成的桥接。实验表明PlanAudio在语音、声音及其复合场景中均优于现有基线。

<h3 id="291">[291] I Hear, Therefore I Trust: A Socio-Technical Investigation of Humans as Synthetic Speech Detectors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lelia Erscoi, Tomi Kinnunen
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Speech Synthesis, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: Odyssey 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究人类作为合成语音检测者的社会技术环境，通过定位任务实验探索信任线索对检测行为的影响。结果表明，语音类别是检测准确率和感知质量的主要决定因素，完全合成的语音检测率低于随机水平，而质量评分显示隐式辨别能力。揭示了人类检测合成语音的局限性。

<h3 id="292">[292] PromptEmbedder:: Efficient and Transferable Text Embedding via Dual-LLM Soft Prompting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28066](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28066) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu-Che Tsai, Kuan-Yu Chen, Yuan-Hao Chen, Yu-Han Chang, Ching-Yu Tsai, Yu-Hsiang Chuang, Shou-De Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text Embedding, Prompt Engineering, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文针对当前LLM文本嵌入方法（如LoRA）计算效率低且跨架构迁移性差的问题，提出了PromptEmbedder双LLM框架。通过提示LLM生成指令感知的软提示，冻结嵌入LLM，将任务知识解耦到提示LLM中。在MTEB基准上，该方法在保持性能的同时减少了40% GPU内存并加速3.7倍训练。

<h3 id="293">[293] StoryLens: Preference-Aligned Story Rewriting via Context-Aware Narrative Enrichment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28073](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28073) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanwen Cui, Yuting Mei, Yuhang Fu, Dingyi Yang, Qin Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Creative Writing, Preference Optimization, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 本文针对故事改写中仅风格改编无法满足读者偏好的问题，提出了上下文感知叙事丰富的方法。构建了STORYLENSBENCH基准，提出了STORYLENSWRITER两阶段改写模型，结合监督微调和GRPO强化学习。实验表明上下文感知叙事丰富能显著提升个性化故事改写效果。

<h3 id="294">[294] Mind the Gap: Mixtures of Gaussians in Approximate Differential Privacy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28078](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28078) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huikang Liu, Aras Selvi, Wolfram Wiesemann
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Differential Privacy, Mechanism Design
- **Summary**: 本文针对差分隐私中高斯机制的次优性，设计了一类混合高斯机制，通过混合不同均值的高斯分布来降低噪声幅度。推导了满足(ε,δ)-DP的方差条件，并提供了高效算法。在低隐私 regimes下，该机制接近最优，显著缩小了高斯机制的最优性差距。

<h3 id="295">[295] SMILE-Next: Teaching Large Language Models to Detect, Classify, and Reason about Laughter</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28084) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lee Jung-Mok, Kim Sung-Bin, Joohyun Chang, Lee Hyun, Tae-Hyun Oh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Multimodal Learning, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文针对笑声理解的多任务挑战，提出了SMILE-Next数据集和笑声专用大语言模型。包含笑声检测、类型分类和推理三个任务，设计了笑声专用Self-Instruct和混合笑声专家（MoLE）框架。实验表明该方法在多模态LLM基线上显著提升，推动了真实世界笑声理解。

<h3 id="296">[296] Revisiting Change Detection Methods for their Application to Serac Fall Time-Lapse Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28100) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arthur Dérédel, Carlos Crispim-Junior, Pierre Lemaire, Johan Berthet, Laure Tougne Rodet
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Change Detection, Remote Sensing, Environmental Monitoring
- **Summary**: 本文针对气候变化下环境监测中变化检测方法在冰崖崩塌时间延迟监测中的应用挑战，提出了新的子任务“体积变化检测”。通过综述现有方法并引入SeracFallDet数据集，实验发现密集和半密集特征匹配表现出色，而监督方法受限于数据稀缺。揭示了混合方法的潜力。

<h3 id="297">[297] EigeNet: Geometry-Informed Multi-Modal Learning for Few-shot Novel View RIR Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28101) (cross-listed)
