- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Audio Processing, Multimodal Learning, Few-Shot Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/FEAfeatherTHER/EigeNet)
- **Summary**: 本文针对从稀疏观测预测空间变化房间脉冲响应（RIR）的逆问题，提出了EigeNet几何信息多模态框架。核心是交叉视图交替注意力Transformer，结合几何调制模块和辅助损失。在模拟和真实基准上实现了少样本新视角RIR预测的SOTA性能。

<h3 id="298">[298] MIRAGE: Context-Aware Prompt Injection against Mobile GUI Agents via User-Generated Content</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28116) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruoqi Guo, Yi Liu, Gelei Deng, Yiheng Xiong, Yuekang Li, Ying Zhang, Leo Yu Zhang, Lida Zhao, Ji Jie, Yuxiao Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Prompt Injection, GUI Automation, Mobile AI
- **Summary**: 本文针对移动GUI智能体易受用户生成内容中提示注入攻击的问题，提出了MIRAGE流水线，通过将攻击者控制的文本嵌入普通用户内容区域，生成视觉上逼真的对抗样本。实验评估了五个VLM智能体，攻击成功率达23%-30%，且真实感评分优于先前攻击。

<h3 id="299">[299] LegalGraphRAG: Multi-Agent Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Reliable Legal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28120) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zerui Chen, Qinggang Zhang, Zhishang Xiang, Zhimin Wei, Linfeng Gao, Xiao Huang, Zhihong Zhang, Jinsong Su
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Legal AI, RAG, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/LegalGraphRAG)
- **Summary**: 本文针对法律推理中图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）面临的异构知识和证据验证挑战，提出了LegalGraphRAG框架。包含分层法律图和多智能体系统（研究者、审计者、裁决者）。实验表明在法律推理任务上达到SOTA性能。

<h3 id="300">[300] SNARE: Adaptive Scenario Synthesis for Eliciting Overeager Behavior in Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28122) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yubin Qu, Yi Liu, Gelei Deng, Yanjun Zhang, Yuekang Li, Ying Zhang, Leo Yu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文针对编码智能体在完成良性任务时可能超出授权范围（过度行为）的问题，提出了SNARE流水线，通过组合场景片段并使用汤普森采样引导测试预算。在24种过度行为原型上构建了OverEager基准，发现19.51%的良性运行触发过度行为，且框架的影响大于模型。

<h3 id="301">[301] DeltaMCP: Incremental Regeneration via Spec-Aware Transformation for MCP servers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Pujara, Xiaogang Zhu, Hsiang-Ting Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Software Engineering, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文针对企业级API演化导致MCP服务器实现同步困难的问题，提出了DeltaMCP增量再生工具，基于OpenAPI规范差异仅更新受影响的部分。在Azure REST API数据集上评估，结果表明减少了开发开销并提高了维护一致性。

<h3 id="302">[302] DEPART: DEcomposing PARiTy across Multilingual LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28163](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28163) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Manan Uppadhyay, Prashant Kodali, Pranjal Chitale, Reshma Ramaprasad, Himanshu Beniwal, Sunayana Sitaram
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, LLM Evaluation, Fairness
- **Summary**: 本文针对多语言LLM性能差异的原因解释不足的问题，提出了两步贝叶斯分层框架分解多语言性能方差。发现语言特征解释了理解任务79%和推理任务92%的方差，且模型内部与英语的表示相似性是主要预测因子。NLU和推理的方差分布不同。

<h3 id="303">[303] Performance and Explainability Requirements of Evolutionary Algorithms in Real-World Physics-Informed Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28164](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28164) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Helena Stegherr, Michael Heider, Nils Meyer, Tobias Thummerer, Thomas Wendler, Pierre Aublin, Ennio Idrobo-Àvila, Lars Mikelsons, Sebastian Zaunseder, Jörg Hähner
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Computation, Physics-Informed Learning, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 本文针对进化算法在真实世界物理信息优化中表现不足且缺乏可解释性问题，介绍了五个真实物理优化问题，并总结了领域专家对算法性能和可解释性的需求。发现所有专家期望快速收敛和结果解释，但其他需求因问题而异，指出需要弥合研究与实际应用之间的差距。

<h3 id="304">[304] QuITE: Query-Based Irregular Time Series Embedding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28166) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: JungHoon Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Time Series Classification, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Meaningfull9502/QuITE)
- **Summary**: 本文针对不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS）建模中现有方法需要专门架构或插值导致问题，提出了QuITE查询嵌入模块，通过可学习查询令牌聚合不规则观测，无需修改骨干网络。在多个基准上，QuITE平均提升预测54.7%和分类15.8%。

<h3 id="305">[305] FLORO: A Multimodal Geospatial Foundation Model for Ecological Remote Sensing Across Sensors and Scales</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28174](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28174) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jorge L. Rodriguez, Victor Angulo Morales, Areej Alwahas, Mariana Elias Lara, Fida Mohammad Thoker, Kasper Johansen, Bernard Ghanem, Fernando T. Maestre, Matthew F. McCabe
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Foundation Model, Remote Sensing
- **Summary**: 针对现有遥感基础模型依赖大规模预训练数据和固定传感器配置，难以适配生态与环境应用中多平台、多分辨率和多模态观测的问题，提出FLORO多模态地理空间基础模型。采用掩码自编码在异构遥感数据上预训练，并引入可用性感知输入统一异构传感器空间。在PANGAEA基准上，FLORO在冻结编码器协议下在场景分类、分割和回归任务中取得强且稳定的迁移性能，分割性能仅次于预训练数据量大两个数量级的模型，且回归任务稳健。

<h3 id="306">[306] BenGER: Benchmarking LLM Systems on Subsumption-Based Legal Reasoning in German Law</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28183) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Nagl, Ann-Kristin Mayrhofer, Martin Heidebach, Aleyna Koçak, Anne Zettelmeier, Elly Breu, Angelina Greiner, Sofija Milijas, Matthias Grabmair
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 针对德国法律中基于涵摄的法律推理任务缺乏评估基准的问题，提出了BenGER数据集，包含596个考试级法律案例任务和531个简短教义推理任务。评估了12个当代LLM系统，并引入LLM-as-a-Judge框架与人工评分交叉验证。结果表明闭源旗舰模型在所有语料库上领先，且人-AI协同创作显著优于纯人工表现。

<h3 id="307">[307] Visualizing Latent Phase Structures in Locomotion Policies: A Multi-Environment Study with Temporal Feature Extension</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28186](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28186) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daisuke Yasui, Toshitaka Matuki, Hiroshi Sato
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Motion Planning, Locomotion Control
- **Summary**: 针对深度强化学习在运动控制策略中内部运动阶段结构难以可视化的问题，提出一种从环境交互轨迹中揭示隐式运动阶段结构的框架。通过扩展聚类特征并引入抑制自转移的聚类数确定方法，在多个环境中成功识别出比现有方法更清晰、规则性更强的阶段结构。

<h3 id="308">[308] Whose Name Comes Up? III: Persona Prompting Effects in LLM-Based Scholar Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28187) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Annabella Sánchez-Guzmán, Lukas Eberhard, Denis Helic, Lisette Espín-Noboa
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Mitigation, Benchmark, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对LLM作为学者推荐系统时存在的评估基准单一的问题，提出了一个基准来解耦模型选择和提示设计的影响。审计了43个LLM，通过变化人物提示和上下文评估推荐质量。结果表明模型选择驱动基本技术质量，上下文影响事实性和性别平等性，地点影响多样性。

<h3 id="309">[309] Pruning and Distilling Mixture-of-Experts into Dense Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28207) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junhyuck Kim, Jihun Yun, Haechan Kim, Gyeongman Kim, Joonghyun Bae, Jaewoong Cho
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Knowledge Distillation, Mixture-of-Experts, Pruning
- **Summary**: 针对MoE模型在内存受限部署时的局限，提出了首个将训练好的MoE模型转换为标准稠密架构的系统性框架。通过专家评分、选择和分组，拼接为稠密前馈网络，并通过知识蒸馏精炼。在多个模型上评估了多种方法，发现多样性感知评分方法最优，在参数相当的情况下平均下游准确率提高6.3个百分点。

<h3 id="310">[310] SmartIterator: Visual Analytics Workflows for Supervising Unsupervised Data Grouping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28219) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gennady Andrienko, Natalia Andrienko
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Visual Analytics, Clustering, Unsupervised Learning, Topic Modeling
- **Summary**: 针对无监督分组方法参数选择与评估缺乏引导的问题，提出SmartIterator可视化分析方法。将参数扫描过程中的完整分组序列作为一阶分析对象，为每种方法族提供六阶段工作流，并通过IteraScope协调可视化展示。在三个示例上展示了工作流能帮助分析师理解数据结构。

<h3 id="311">[311] VidPrism: Heterogeneous Mixture of Experts for Image-to-Video Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28229) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Lin, Chuanming Wang, Huadong Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: CVPR2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Lrrrr549/VidPrism.git)
- **Summary**: 针对视频理解中图像到视频迁移学习时传统MoE专家同质化的问题，提出VidPrism异质时域MoE框架。通过部署功能专门化的专家，引入内容感知多速率采样模块和动态双向融合机制。在多个视频识别基准上取得了最先进性能，并有效促进了专家专业化。

<h3 id="312">[312] IRDS: Interpretable RLVR Data Selection via Verifier-Coupled Sparse Autoencoder Coverage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28247) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Li, Mingxu Zhang, Dazhong Shen, Ying Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Data Selection, Sparse Autoencoder, Interpretability, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对RLVR数据效率低的问题，提出了IRDS可解释数据选择方法。基于稀疏自编码器簇选择实例，引入验证器耦合的覆盖目标，通过贪婪对数行列式最大化求解。在多个模型和数学推理基准上取得最高准确率，超过最强基线最多4.0个百分点，且运行成本低。

<h3 id="313">[313] GUI Agents for Continual Game Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixu Huang, Bo Li, Na Li, Zhe Wang, Kaijie Chen, Haonan Ge, Qingyi Si, Yuanzhe Shen, Ruihan Yang, Guangjing Wang, Hongcheng Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.HC
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Code Generation, Game AI, Agent, Game Generation
- **Summary**: 针对现有游戏生成方法忽略交互级错误的问题，提出使用GUI智能体作为评估者和游戏测试者。引入PlaytestArena环境和Play2Code框架，其中游戏智能体和GUI智能体在共享内存中交互。Play2Code达到66.8%的规则通过率，比单次和智能体编码基线分别提高37.1和14.6个百分点。

<h3 id="314">[314] PrunePath: Towards Highly Structured Sparse Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28283) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhexuan Gu, Zixun Fu, Yancheng Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Pruning, Mixture-of-Experts, Structured Sparsity
- **Summary**: 针对现有剪枝方法难以将稀疏性转化为硬件推理增益的问题，提出PrunePath框架。通过softmax归一化路由和累积质量阈值实现自适应专家数量和稀疏控制。在多个评估中取得有利稀疏-性能权衡，并实现KV缓存解码加速。

<h3 id="315">[315] ProRL: Effective Reinforcement Learning for Proactive Recommendation via Rectified Policy Gradient Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28293](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28293) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongru Hou, Tiehua Mei, Denghui Geng, Jinhui Huang, Ao Xu, Hengrui Chen, Jiaqing Liang, Deqing Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Reinforcement Learning, Proactive Recommendation
- **Venue**: ICML 2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hongruhou89/ProRL)
- **Summary**: 针对主动推荐系统中策略梯度估计存在偏差和高方差的问题，提出ProRL框架，包含逐步奖励中心化和位置特定优势估计。在三个真实数据集上显著优于现有最先进方法。

<h3 id="316">[316] How Far Can Disaggregation Go? A Design-Space Exploration of Attention-FFN Disaggregation for Efficient MoE LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28302](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28302) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanjiang Wu, Abhimanyu Rajeshkumar Bambhaniya, Sarbartha Banerjee, Tuhin Khare, Sudarshan Srinivasan, Suvinay Subramanian, Souvik Kundu, Madhu Kumar, Midhilesh Elavazhagan, William Won, Amir Yazdanbakhsh, Tushar Krishna
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Mixture-of-Experts, System Design, GPU Computing
- **Summary**: 针对MoE模型推理中不同算子资源需求不同的挑战，系统研究了注意力-FFN分离的设计空间。通过融合内核测量和网络仿真评估了AFD在大规模部署中的收益。在严格延迟约束下，AFD在DeepSeek-V3.2上维持约4k tokens/s的吞吐量。

<h3 id="317">[317] Revisiting Anthropomorphic Reflection Markers in Large Language Model Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28305](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28305) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yahan Yu, Noa Nakanishi, Fei Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Prompt Engineering, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对LLM推理中人形化反思标记的机制不明确及可能导致过度思考的问题，通过干预抑制这些标记并分析影响。结果表明标记并非必需，抑制后性能可能保持或提升，且抑制标记不一定消除反思行为。

<h3 id="318">[318] Routing-Aligned Fine-Tuning for Multilingual Downstream Tasks in Mixture-of-Experts Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28306) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guanzhi Deng, Kuan Wu, Haibo Wang, Shing Yin Wong, Sichun Luo, Linqi Song
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Multilingual Learning, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对MoE模型微调时忽略路由结构的问题，发现中间层路由差异预测任务性能差距，提出RA-MoE框架。通过分类示例、识别相关专家和路由对齐损失，在多个模型和语言上一致优于标准SFT和强基线。

<h3 id="319">[319] Hybrid Neural World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pranav Lakshmanan, Paras Chopra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA
- **Tags**: World Model, Physics-Informed Learning, PDE Solver, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 针对神经替代模型在剧烈动力学事件上失效的问题，提出混合神经世界模型。单网络预测任意未来状态，隐式编码误差图集中在激波等区域。纯代理模式CPU加速26-72倍，误差门控回退模式将残差误差减半。

<h3 id="320">[320] Identifying Explicit Parsimonious Piece-wise Polynomial Relationships in Industrial time-series: Application to manipulator robots</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28320) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mazen Alamir, Sacha Clavel
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Anomaly Detection, Robotics, Symbolic Regression
- **Summary**: 针对工业时间序列中识别精简显式分段多项式关系的问题，提出一种利用隐式关系推导显式分段表示的算法。在六轴机械臂逆模型识别上验证，并在四轴机器人上展示了优于DNN的泛化能力。

<h3 id="321">[321] Multi-Agent LLM-based Metamorphic Testing for REST APIs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28321](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28321) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shehroz Khan, Abdullah Mughees, Gaadha Sudheerbabu, Tanwir Ahmad, Dragos Truscan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: COMPSAC2026
- **Summary**: 针对REST API测试中的Oracle问题，提出基于LLM多智能体工作流的蜕变测试方法ARMeta。该方法通过多智能体协作识别蜕变测试场景并自动执行。实验表明ARMeta能补充现有场景测试方法。

<h3 id="322">[322] Learning the Error Patterns of Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28328) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinwoo Kim, Taylor Berg-KirkPatrick, Loris D&#39;Antoni
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型在生成带约束的代码时存在特定错误模式。提出前缀过滤器（prefix filters）和Palla算法来学习这些模式，并通过约束采样提升代码编译成功率。在TypeScript生成任务中，Palla将编译率提升超过60%。

<h3 id="323">[323] Improving Evaluation of Recombination-based Cartesian Genetic Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28353) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Duy Long Tran, Anja Jankovic, Marie Anastacio, Holger Hoos, Roman Kalkreuth
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Evolutionary Algorithm
- **Venue**: GGP Workshop at GECCO'26
- **Summary**: 针对传统仅用变异的笛卡尔遗传规划，研究重组算子的性能。通过超参数优化，在符号回归基准上显示重组算子经调优后能提升性能。

<h3 id="324">[324] Score Based Error Correcting Code Decoder</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28358](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28358) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alon Helvits, Eliya Nachmani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Error-Correcting Code, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有纠错码软解码面临挑战，提出基于分数的解码器SB-ECC，利用连续时间去噪将解码转化为ODE过程。在42个设置中39个取得最佳误码率，平均信噪比增益0.17dB。

<h3 id="325">[325] CLANE: Continual Learning of Actions on Neuromorphic Hardware from Event Cameras</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28387](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28387) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elvin Hajizada, Michael Neumeier, Edward Paxon Frady, Yulia Sandamirskaya, Axel von Arnim, Bing Li, Eyke Hüllermeier
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Neuromorphic Computing, Action Recognition
- **Summary**: 面向增强现实和机器人应用，提出首个基于神经形态硬件的连续学习动作识别系统CLANE。结合脉冲CNN和片上学习，在THU E-ACT-50数据集上达到70.4%准确率，能耗降低100倍以上。

<h3 id="326">[326] ADWIN: Adaptive Windows for Horizon-Aware On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28396) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kun Liang, Chenming Tang, Clive Bai, Weijie Liu, Saiyong Yang, Yunfang Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对策略蒸馏中全轨迹训练的低效问题，提出自适应窗口框架ADWIN。通过在线决策轨迹长度，在数学和代码推理任务上实现高达4.1倍的训练成本降低，同时保持相似或更好精度。

<h3 id="327">[327] VITAL: Visual-Semantic Dual Supervision for Enhanced and Interpretable Latent Reasoning in Medical MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28422](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28422) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiaoru Li, Shaotian Liang, Jintao Chen, Haoran Sun, Yuxiang Cai, Jianwei Yin, Yankai Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Medical AI, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 医疗多模态大语言模型缺乏可解释的潜在推理，提出VITAL框架，通过视觉-语义双监督增强潜在推理。构建6.1万数据集覆盖9种模态，在7个基准上达到与万亿参数模型竞争的最优结果。

<h3 id="328">[328] Anomaly as Non-Conformity via Training-Free Graph Laplacian Energy Minimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jungwook Seo, Minjeong Kim, Younkwan Lee, Seungho Shin, Sungyong Baik
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Unsupervised Learning
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 无监督异常检测中，相似度度量难以捕捉对正常流形结构的违反。提出ANoCo，通过无训练拉普拉斯图能量优化将异常评分建模为不一致性代价，在基准上取得强AUROC和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="329">[329] Bayesian Gated Non-Negative Contrastive Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peng Cui, Jiahao Zhang, Lijie Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Contrastive Learning, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Cui-Peng-624/BayesNCL)
- **Summary**: 对比学习表示高度纠缠，源于确定性相似度导致的优化冲突。提出贝叶斯门控非负对比学习BayesNCL，通过变分推断实现特征选择，在ImageNet-100上语义一致性提升142.1%。

<h3 id="330">[330] BiasEdit: A Training-Free Bias-Detect-and-Edit Framework for Learning Fair Visual Classifiers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28450) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jungwook Seo, Yoonsik Park, Changmin Lee, Sungyong Baik
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, Fairness, Image Classification
- **Venue**: The Web Conference 2026
- **Summary**: Web数据中的偏见影响图像分类器公平性。提出BiasEdit框架，自动检测并编辑偏见属性以生成去偏数据集，无需人工标注，取得最先进去偏性能。

<h3 id="331">[331] The Cases LJP Never Sees: Prosecution Decision Prediction for More Complete Criminal Liability Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28464) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyu Lu, Qi Wei, Peishuo Zheng, Jie Zhang, Hui Huang, Qianru Wang, Chuan Xiao, Jianbin Qin, Shuyuan Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 法律判决预测忽略未起诉案件，存在盲区。提出起诉决定预测任务PDP及基准PDP-Bench，涵盖4630个真实决定。实验显示LLM在PDP上表现显著低于LJP，简单RLVR干预无效。

<h3 id="332">[332] SSR3D-LLM: Structured Spatial Reasoning via Latent Steps for Fine-Grained Grounding in Unified 3D-LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28490](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28490) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Li, Ziyi Liu, Weijie Shi, Long Chen, Jiajie Xu, Xiaofang Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Visual Grounding, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 统一3D大语言模型在细粒度目标定位中脆弱。提出SSR3D-LLM，通过结构化空间推理步骤和几何感知评分器提升候选排序。在多个基准上达成统一3D-LLM最佳结果。

<h3 id="333">[333] The Decision to Verify: How Warmth and User Characteristics Shape Reliance on Conversational Agents for Information Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28498) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mert Yazan, Frederik Bungaran Ishak Situmeang, Suzan Verberne
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Human-Computer Interaction, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 用户对对话式AI存在过度依赖。通过实验发现即使提供事实核查渠道，依赖仍然存在；验证行为主要由用户感知驱动，而温暖对话风格间接增加错误同意。

<h3 id="334">[334] Functional Entropy: Predicting Functional Correctness in LLM-Generated Code with Uncertainty Quantification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28500) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dylan Bouchard, Mohit Singh Chauhan, Zeya Ahmad, Ho-Kyeong Ra
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 代码生成中不确定性量化方法未经充分探索。提出功能等价方法及功能熵，替换自然语言推理的语义等价，在15个模型-基准组合中11个达到最佳AUROC。

<h3 id="335">[335] Efficient and Scalable Provenance Tracking for LLM-Generated Code Snippets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrea Gurioli, Davide D&#39;Ascenzo, Federico Pennino, Maurizio Gabbrielli, Stefano Zacchiroli
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Plagiarism Detection, Code Search
- **Summary**: LLM生成代码的溯源追踪在大规模语料下不可行。提出混合两阶段溯源管线HST，结合向量搜索与指纹匹配，在百万级片段上实现对数复杂度的高精度追踪。

<h3 id="336">[336] Learning Theory of the SVRG: Generalization and Convergence Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28513](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28513) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunwen Lei, Zimeng Wang, Xiaoming Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Generalization Bound
- **Summary**: 方差缩减方法SVRG的泛化行为缺乏理论分析。通过算法稳定性建立首个非平凡的泛化界，在凸和强凸情况下得到最优过剩风险界。

<h3 id="337">[337] Do LLMs Favor Their Providers? Measuring Vertical Integration Bias in Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28515](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28515) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Melih Catal, Alex Wolf, Tiago Ferreiro Matos, Pooja Rani, Harald Gall
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 论文研究大型语言模型在代码生成中是否偏向其提供商生态系统（垂直整合偏见）。提出了VIBench基准和VIB测量方法，评估了10个提供商关联模型。结果显示，直接生成中存在显著偏见（高达+18.8个百分点），智能体工作流中偏见放大至+39.2个百分点，且早期选择会持续影响。

<h3 id="338">[338] Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum is Algorithmically Stable</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28517](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28517) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunwen Lei, Zimeng Wang, Xiaoming Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Generalization Bound
- **Summary**: 论文研究了带动量的随机梯度下降（SGDM）的泛化性能。通过算法稳定性分析了SGDM的泛化界，建立了针对光滑凸问题的平均模型稳定性界，并获得最优过剩人口风险界。结论是动量不一定会降低泛化。

<h3 id="339">[339] Token Optimization Strategies for LLM-Based Oracle-to-PostgreSQL Migration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28557) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oleg Grynets, Dmytro Babarytskyi, Vasyl Lyashkevych
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Inference, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 论文针对LLM驱动的Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移中的高token消耗问题，形式化和评估了12种token优化策略。结果表明，温和的上下文剪枝保持语义质量，自适应路由提供了最佳实用折衷，而激进的模式蒸馏导致语义匹配大幅下降。

<h3 id="340">[340] A Multi-dimensional Framework for Evaluating Generalization in EEG Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28563) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Kommineni, Emily Zhou, Kleanthis Avramidis, Tiantian Feng, Shrikanth Narayanan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Foundation Model, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 论文提出评估脑电图基础模型在多维低资源条件下的泛化能力框架。实验发现，在长窗口任务（如睡眠阶段预测）中基础模型表现更好，但在短窗口BCI任务中监督模型相当。基础模型对短窗口和通道受限场景鲁棒性有限。

<h3 id="341">[341] Verified Misguidance: Measuring Structural Citation Failures in Search-Augmented LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28565) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongsik Seo, Wooseok Jeong, Eunyoung Kim, Hyeonseo Jang, Dongha Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 论文构建了CITETRACE数据集和三维评估框架，测量搜索增强LLM的引用失败。发现30.6%的引用扭曲来源，27.1%来自域不适当来源，高达96%的用户遇到至少一次误导性引用。提供商差异解释了大部分引用质量方差。

<h3 id="342">[342] Semantic Optimal Transport for Sparse Autoencoder Feature Matching and Circuit Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28567) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tue M. Cao, Nguyen Do, My T. Thai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Mechanistic Interpretability, Optimal Transport
- **Summary**: 论文提出基于分布框架的语义最优传输方法，用于稀疏自编码器特征匹配和电路压缩。通过激活加权分布和Wasserstein距离，实现跨层特征比较。方法优于基线和LLM基线，自动压缩电路为可解释超节点。

<h3 id="343">[343] Efficient Pre-Training of LLMs through Truncated SVD Layers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28573](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28573) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaivan Kamali, Kajetan Schweighofer, Hormoz Shahrzad, Olivier Francon, Babak Hodjat, Risto Miikkulainen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Model Compression, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 论文提出TSVD框架，通过低秩和正交权重矩阵实现高效LLM预训练。使用谱能量启发自适应秩选择和缓存机制保持正交性。实验表明，TSVD在减少计算量的同时匹配或超越全参数基线。

<h3 id="344">[344] SARAD: LLM-Based Safety-Aware Hybrid Reinforcement Learning with Collision Prediction for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28583](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28583) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kangyu Wu, Peng Cui, Guoxi Chen, Ya Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Safe RL, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: IJCNN 2026
- **Summary**: 论文提出SARAD框架，结合LLM和深度强化学习实现安全自动驾驶。用RAG增强的LLM指导替代DRL随机探索，注意力判别器整合先验知识，碰撞预测器提升安全性。在Highway-Env模拟器中显著改进。

<h3 id="345">[345] Technical Report: Exploring the Emerging Threats of the Agent Skill Ecosystem</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28588](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28588) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luca Beurer-Kellner, Aleksei Kudrinskii, Marco Milanta, Kristian Bonde Nielsen, Hemang Sarkar, Liran Tal
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Security, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 论文分析了3984个AI智能体技能，发现76个恶意负载，13.4%的技能存在严重安全问题。提出威胁分类和攻击模式，强调自动安全分析的必要性。

<h3 id="346">[346] Models That Know How Evaluations Are Designed Score Safer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28591) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katharina Deckenbach, Haritz Puerto, Jonas Geiping, Sahar Abdelnabi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark Contamination
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/compass-group-tue/arxiv2026_evaluation_meta_knowledge)
- **Summary**: 论文提出评估元知识概念，即模型从训练文本中学习到评估结构特征导致安全评分提高。通过微调模型接触合成评估特征文档，发现模型在安全基准上更安全，且这种效应独立于显式记忆或评估意识。

<h3 id="347">[347] Thermodynamic properties of chemically disordered compounds via AI-driven estimation of partition function with the PULSE method</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28594](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28594) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Baptiste Bernard, Luca Messina, Eiji Kawasaki, Emeric Bourasseau
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Physics-Informed Learning, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 论文改进PULSE方法，用AI驱动估计化学无序化合物的热力学性质。以2D Ising模型验证，能高精度高效重现平均性质，相比传统蒙特卡洛方法更有效。

<h3 id="348">[348] Position: Retire the "Positive Backdoor" Label -- Secret Alignment Requires Strict and Systematic Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28597](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28597) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianwei Li, Jung-Eun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, AI Safety, LLM Security
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 论文主张废弃“正向后门”标签，改用“秘密对齐”，并需要严格系统评估。评估了三个代表性应用在六个属性上的表现，发现触发性为映射的脆弱性，呼吁社区标准评估。

<h3 id="349">[349] Evaluating the Realism of LLM-powered Social Agents: A Case Study of Reactions to Spanish Online News</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28598](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28598) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alejandro Buitrago López, Alberto Ortega Pastor, Javier Pastor-Galindo, José A. Ruipérez-Valiente
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, LLM Agent, Hate Speech Detection
- **Summary**: 论文评估LLM驱动的社交智能体生成对西班牙在线新闻反应的现实性。使用Hatemedia数据集，比较真实和合成反应在仇恨言论、情感和语义对齐上的差异。发现现成模型是糟糕的代理，微调改进不均匀。

<h3 id="350">[350] Online Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting via Uncertainty-Driven Dual-Expert Calibration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28603](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28603) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haonan Wen, Hanyang Chen, Songhe Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Uncertainty Estimation, Online Learning
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HaonanWen/Under-Cali)
- **Summary**: 论文提出Under-Cali框架用于在线不规则多变量时间序列预测。包括不确定性估计器、双专家校准模块和自适应路由模块。在基准测试上以低计算成本实现一致改进。

<h3 id="351">[351] Mining Multi-Modality Spatio-Temporal Cues for Video Important Person Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28604) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao Wang, Minglei Yang, Bin Yang, Wenke Huang, Zheng Wang, Xin Xu, Mang Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multi-Modal Fusion
- **Summary**: 论文提出视频重要人物识别任务（VIP），构建Temporal-VIP数据集，开发VIP-Net框架，包含社交线索编码器、时间重要性校正器和排名。实验准确率67.3%，显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="352">[352] Measuring Form and Function in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28616) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Héctor Javier Vázquez Martínez, Charles Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Language Modeling, LLM Evaluation, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 论文引入儿童语言习得定量指标评估语言模型，关注英语限定词的句法和语用知识。提出Contextual Alternative Choice (CAC)提示方法。发现大型模型能同时满足形式和功能基准，但较小模型不能。

<h3 id="353">[353] Blind PRNG Hijacking: An Undetectable Integrity-Preserving Attack Against LLM Watermarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyang You, Huilong He, Xiaoke Yang, Xuxing Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Watermarking, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对LLM水印生成中伪随机数生成器(PRNG)不被信任的安全漏洞，提出SeedHijack攻击，在不改变输出文本的情况下替换PRNG以偏向绿表选择。实验表明攻击可逃避6个检测器并将水印z分数提高至2.42倍，量子随机数生成器可完全防御。该攻击展现了PRNG完整性对于加密内容来源系统的安全必要性。

<h3 id="354">[354] The Attentional White Bear Effect in Transformer Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rebecca Ramnauth, Brian Scassellati
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Alignment, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 研究指令抑制是否真正减少模型内部表示中的禁止概念，发现即使成功避免生成，禁止概念在隐藏表示中仍可恢复，并影响注意力路由和下游生成。结果表明行为对齐与表示对齐之间存在差距。

<h3 id="355">[355] Sense Representations Are Inducible Interfaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28669) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jan Christian Blaise Cruz, Alham Fikri Aji
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Word Sense Disambiguation, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jcblaisecruz02/acros)
- **Summary**: 提出ACROS方法，通过门控残差加法在冻结的预训练解码器LM中诱导显式语义感知路径。在SmolLM2-360M上，ACROS在保留基础LM质量的同时，支持零样本词义消歧（F1 64.95）、低KL词汇引导和跨语言适应。

<h3 id="356">[356] AI in the Workplace: The Impact of AI on Perceived Job Decency and Meaningfulness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28680) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kuntal Ghosh, Marc Hassenzahl, Shadan Sadeghian
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, Social Impact, AI Ethics
- **Venue**: CSCW 2026
- **Summary**: 通过采访24名IT、服务和医疗领域员工，研究AI对工作满意度的影响，发现AI对不同职业领域的工作体面感和意义感影响不同。IT和医疗预期工作时间等体面方面满意度增加，但社会形象等意义方面因误解而下降；服务工人则预期工作时间无改善但社会地位提升。

<h3 id="357">[357] Misalignment Between Backpropagation and the Hierarchy of Brain Responses to Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28693) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joséphine Raugel, Maximilian Seitzer, Marc Szafraniec, Huy V. Vo, Jérémy Rapin, Patrick Labatut, Piotr Bojanowski, Valentin Wyart, Jean-Rémi King
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuroscience, Cognitive Science, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 使用fMRI和MEG记录人类对自然图像的脑反应，发现预训练模型的反向传播梯度虽能预测脑信号，但其时空组织与大脑层级不一致，表明深度学习与大脑可能依赖不同学习机制。

<h3 id="358">[358] Deep Learning Strain Estimation: Is Physics-Based Simulation the Solution?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28697) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thierry Judge, Nicolas Duchateau, Andreas Østvik, Khuram Faraz, Anders Austlid Taskén, Sigve Karlsen, Thor Edvardsen, Harald Brunvand, Md Abulkalam Azad, Havard Dalen, Bjørnar Grenne, Gabriel Kiss, Pierre-Yves Courand, Lasse Lovstakken, Pierre-Marc Jodoin, Olivier Bernard
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Simulation, Image Registration
- **Summary**: 针对超声心动图心肌应变估计中区域应变准确度低的问题，提出结合散斑去相关模拟和迭代优化的仿真策略，生成1478个视频的光真实感数据集，训练运动估计算法。在全局应变变异性上达到1.42%，优于临床参考的1.78%。

<h3 id="359">[359] A Fresh Look at Lamarckian Evolution and the Baldwin Effect</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28703) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Inès Benito, Johannes F. Lutzeyer, Benjamin Doerr
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.DS; math.OC
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Algorithm, Combinatorial Optimization, Graph Learning
- **Venue**: PPSN 2026
- **Summary**: 通过图优化基准GraphBench上的实验和理论分析，重新评估拉马克进化、鲍德温效应与达尔文进化在进化算法中的表现。实验显示鲍德温和拉马克进化一致优于达尔文进化，并接近专用求解器性能；理论证明了渐近时间复杂度顺序。

<h3 id="360">[360] Towards Reliable Multilingual LLMs-as-a-Judge: An Empirical Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28710) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Irune Zubiaga, Aitor Soroa, Rodrigo Agerri
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Multilingual Learning, Low-Resource NLP
- **Summary**: 探索开发多语言LLM评估器的策略，系统分析英语、西班牙语和巴斯克语（高、中、低资源语言），考虑指令翻译、单语与多语监督、模型大小等因素。结果表明，在有域内数据时，微调的小模型可达与专有模型相当的性能；零样本大模型在域外更有效。

<h3 id="361">[361] IPO-Mine: A Toolkit and Dataset for Section-Structured Analysis of Long, Multimodal IPO Documents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28714) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michael Galarnyk, Siddharth Lohani, Vidhyakshaya Kannan, Sagnik Nandi, Aman Patel, Liqin Ye, Arnav Hiray, Rutwik Routu, Prasun Banerjee, Siddhartha Somani, Sudheer Chava
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, Multimodal Learning, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 针对IPO文件长度超50万token且缺乏结构的问题，构建IPO-Toolkit和IPO-Dataset，包含1994-2026年超过10万份IPO文件及7.6万张图像。建立财务图表评估任务，发现多模态模型与专家判断存在对齐挑战。

<h3 id="362">[362] MemTrace: Tracing and Attributing Errors in Large Language Model Memory Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28732) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinle Deng, Ruobin Zhong, Hujin Peng, Xiaoben Lu, Yanzhe Wu, Guang Li, Buqiang Xu, Yunzhi Yao, Jizhan Fang, Haoliang Cao, Junjie Guo, Yuan Yuan, Ziqing Ma, Yuanqiang Yu, Rui Hu, Baohua Dong, Hangcheng Zhu, Ningyu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, Memory Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/MemTrace)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM记忆系统不可靠且难调试的问题，提出MemTrace框架，将记忆管道转化为可执行记忆演化图，实现错误追踪与归因。构建MemTraceBench基准，并引入自动归因方法，通过提示优化形成闭环系统，端任务性能提升高达7.62%。

<h3 id="363">[363] BIRDNet: Mining and Encoding Boolean Implication Knowledge Graphs as Interpretable Deep Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28739) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tirtharaj Dash
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Graph Neural Network, Knowledge Graph
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MAHI-Group/BIRDNet)
- **Summary**: 针对表格数据中布尔蕴含关系，提出BIRDNet方法，通过挖掘隐含关系构建有向图并编码为稀疏神经网路。在转录组和蛋白质组基准上，BIRDNet与最强密集基线AUROC差距在0.02以内，但活跃参数减少96倍，且第一层规则可恢复已知生物学特征。

<h3 id="364">[364] Reverse Probing: Supervised Token-level Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models in Clinical Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28740) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bushi Xiao, Sarvesh Soni, Daisy Zhe Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对临床文本中LLM的不确定性量化，提出Reverse Probing方法，从预标注摘要中提取内部激活信号实现token级不确定性估计。在两个临床数据集上，AUPRC最高提升4倍，且降低推理时间和计算成本。

<h3 id="365">[365] Preference-Shaped Expected Hypervolume and R2 Improvement: Exact Computation and Monotonicity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28746) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michael T.M. Emmerich
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Bayesian Optimization, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Summary**: 研究贝叶斯多目标优化中偏好形状的期望改进准则，分析超体积指标和R2指标的几何差异。证明精确积分R2改进在一般情况下不是普通超体积，而是标量化空间体积，并给出相应的算法。

<h3 id="366">[366] Extrapolative Weight Averaging Reveals Correctness-Efficiency Frontiers in Code RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28751](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28751) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kunhao Zheng, Pierre Chambon, Juliette Decugis, Jonas Gehring, Taco Cohen, Benjamin Negrevergne, Gabriel Synnaeve
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 在代码强化学习中，线性插值与外推权重平均可揭示正确性与效率之间的帕累托前沿。在外推下，前沿扩展到新检查点，且在不同推理设置和模型规模下一致。集成外推权重平均在LCB/hard上比最佳单检查点pass@250提高3.3%。

<h3 id="367">[367] Rethinking Memory as Continuously Evolving Connectivity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jizhan Fang, Buqiang Xu, Zhixian Wang, Haoliang Cao, Xinle Deng, Baohua Dong, Hangcheng Zhu, Ruohui Huang, Gang Yu, Ying Wei, Guozhou Zheng, Feiyu Xiong, Haofen Wang, Huajun Chen, Ningyu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA; cs.MM
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Graph Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem)
- **Summary**: 针对记忆增强LLM代理在动态环境中脆弱的问题，提出FluxMem框架，将记忆建模为异构图并通过三阶段逐步优化拓扑。在LoCoMo、Mind2Web和GAIA基准上取得一致最先进性能，展现强适应性和泛化能力。

<h3 id="368">[368] Learn from Weaknesses: Automated Domain Specialization for Small Computer-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28775](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28775) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suji Kim, Kangsan Kim, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, LLM Agent, Domain Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对小型计算机使用代理在特定领域表现弱的问题，提出LearnWeak框架，利用更强参考代理识别学生弱点并合成针对性任务。在OSWorld上，平均提升11.6和11.1个百分点，优于现有方法。

<h3 id="369">[369] Do Agents Need Semantic Metadata? A Comparative Study in Agentic Data Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28787](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28787) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiyu Chen, Tarfah Alrashed, Alon Halevy, Natasha Noy
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Semantic Search
- **Summary**: 问题：随着LLM能够浏览非结构化网页，语义元数据对于智能体数据发现是否仍然必要？方法：对比分析基线智能体（搜索开放网页）和语义智能体（利用schema.org语料库）的性能，采用LLM作为评估器，基于FAIR原则进行评价。结果：语义智能体在检索可执行数据方面精确度更高（元数据丰富页面提高44.9%，可机读下载页面提高46.6%），而基线智能体覆盖更广但存在“最后一公里”失效。

<h3 id="370">[370] Skill-Conditioned Gated Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28791) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiazhen Huang, Xiao Chen, Xiao Luo, Yong Dai, Senkang Hu, Yuzhi Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation, Self-Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/walawalagoose/SGSD)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有自蒸馏方法假设特权信息可靠，但来自经验技能库的信息可能不相关或误导。方法：提出技能条件门控自蒸馏（SGSD），利用技能-错误对构建多教师池，通过验证器验证教师极性，并采用稳健的门控目标进行蒸馏。结果：在多项数学推理基准上，SGSD持续优于GRPO，并与答案条件OPSD相当；例如在Qwen3-1.7B上平均提升6.2%和1.7%。

<h3 id="371">[371] OmniVerifier-M1: Multimodal Meta-Verifier with Explicit Structured Recalibration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28805) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinchen Zhang, Bowei Liu, Jiale Liu, Chufan Shi, Yizhen Zhang, Junhong Liu, Youliang Zhang, Zhiheng Li, Yujiu Yang, Ling Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Cominclip/OmniVerifier)
- **Summary**: 问题：多模态大语言模型的视觉输出需要可靠且细粒度的验证。方法：研究多模态元验证，发现符号验证器输出（如边界框）优于文本解释，并采用解耦强化学习目标分别优化二元判断和元验证，训练出OmniVerifier-M1。结果：OmniVerifier-M1提供稳健的验证和细粒度错误定位，并支持动态区域级自修正的智能代理生成系统。

<h3 id="372">[372] Beyond Binary: Sim-to-Real Dexterous Manipulation with Physics-Grounded Contact Representation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28812](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28812) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahe Pan, Stelian Coros, Jitendra Malik, Toru Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sim-to-Real, Dexterous Manipulation, Tactile Sensing
- **Summary**: 问题：接触丰富的操作中，仿真到现实的差距阻碍了密集触觉信息的使用，现有方法将触觉数据简化为粗粒度特征。方法：提出基于物理的接触表示——压力中心（CoP），并设计基于可微动力学的传感器标定方案。结果：CoP条件策略在五指手上实现了零样本仿真到现实迁移，在插销入孔和球平衡任务中优于二值接触和原始触觉基线。

<h3 id="374">[374] Tell Me a Story! Narrative-Driven XAI with Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="375">[375] Apple Intelligence Foundation Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.21075](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21075) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tom Gunter, Zirui Wang, Chong Wang, Ruoming Pang, Andy Narayanan, Aonan Zhang, Bowen Zhang, Chen Chen, Chung-Cheng Chiu, David Qiu, Deepak Gopinath, Dian Ang Yap, Dong Yin, Feng Nan, Floris Weers, Guoli Yin, Haoshuo Huang, Jianyu Wang, Jiarui Lu, John Peebles, Ke Ye, Mark Lee, Nan Du, Qibin Chen, Quentin Keunebroek, Sam Wiseman, Syd Evans, Tao Lei, Vivek Rathod, Xiang Kong, Xianzhi Du, Yanghao Li, Yongqiang Wang, Yuan Gao, Zaid Ahmed, Zhaoyang Xu, Zhiyun Lu, Al Rashid, Albin Madappally Jose, Alec Doane, Alfredo Bencomo, Allison Vanderby, Andrew Hansen, Ankur Jain, Anupama Mann Anupama, Areeba Kamal, Bugu Wu, Carolina Brum, Charlie Maalouf, Chinguun Erdenebileg, Chris Dulhanty, Daniel Parilla, Dominik Moritz, Doug Kang, Eduardo Jimenez, Evan Ladd, Fangping Shi, Felix Bai, Frank Chu, Fred Hohman, Hadas Kotek, Hannah Gillis Coleman, Jane Li, Jeffrey Bigham, Jeffery Cao, Jeff Lai, Jessica Cheung, Jiulong Shan, Joe Zhou, John Li, Jun Qin, Karanjeet Singh, Karla Vega, Kelvin Zou, Laura Heckman, Lauren Gardiner, Margit Bowler, Maria Cordell, Meng Cao, Nicole Hay, Nilesh Shahdadpuri, Otto Godwin, Pranay Dighe, Pushyami Rachapudi, Ramsey Tantawi, Roman Frigg, Sam Davarnia, Sanskruti Shah, Saptarshi Guha, Sasha Sirovica, Shen Ma, Shuang Ma, Simon Wang, Sulgi Kim, Suma Jayaram, Vaishaal Shankar, Varsha Paidi, Vivek Kumar, Xin Wang, Xin Zheng

<h3 id="376">[376] Heterogeneous Causal Discovery of Repeated Undesirable Health Outcomes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.11477](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11477) (replaced)

<h3 id="377">[377] Text-Only Data Synthesis for Vision Language Model Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.22655](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22655) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaomin Yu, Wenjie Zhang, Ziyue Qiao, Chengwei Qin, Hui Xiong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Data Synthesis, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 问题：训练视觉语言模型需要大量高质量的图像-文本对，但收集或合成此类数据成本高昂。方法：提出跨集成的三阶段多模态数据合成框架，仅从文本生成合成图像表示，构建Unicorn-1.2M和Unicorn-471K-Instruction数据集。结果：框架消除了对真实图像的依赖，同时保持数据质量和多样性，为VLM训练提供经济的可扩展方案。

<h3 id="378">[378] Domain size asymptotics for Markov logic networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.04192](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04192) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vera Koponen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO; math.LO
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Knowledge Representation, Markov Logic Network
- **Summary**: 问题：马尔可夫逻辑网络在领域规模趋于无穷时分布性质未知。方法：理论分析，针对一元关系给出几乎完整的渐近行为分类，并比较与提升贝叶斯网络的表达能力。结果：证明在温和条件下MLN分布与均匀分布显著不同；MLN和LBN在表示分布上相互不可比较。

<h3 id="379">[379] A Comparative Study of Rule-Based and Data-Driven Approaches in Industrial Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.15848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15848) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Giovanni De Gasperis, Sante Dino Facchini
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Industrial Monitoring, Industrial AI, Expert System, Anomaly Detection
- **Venue**: Advancements in AI From Foundations to Cross-Disciplinary Applications
- **Summary**: 问题：工业监控系统从基于规则向数据驱动范式转变，需要比较两者的优劣。方法：对比研究，分析各自优势、局限和应用场景，提出混合方案。结果：基于规则系统在稳定环境中解释性强、行为确定；数据驱动系统擅长异常检测和预测性维护，但可解释性差；混合是未来方向。

<h3 id="380">[380] RL Squeezes, SFT Expands: A Comparative Study of Reasoning LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.21128](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21128) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kohsei Matsutani, Shota Takashiro, Gouki Minegishi, Takeshi Kojima, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Fine-Tuning, Training Dynamics
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：强化学习（RL）和监督微调（SFT）如何塑造LLM的推理能力，不仅限于准确率。方法：提出新的分析框架，量化推理路径，在轨迹级和步骤级（推理图）进行聚类和度量分析。结果：RL压缩错误轨迹，缩小节点指标衰减率约2.5倍；SFT扩展正确轨迹，衰减率降低至约1/3。解释了当前SFT后RL的两阶段训练为何有效。

<h3 id="381">[381] MetaboT: An LLM-based Multi-Agent Frameworkfor Interactive Analysis of Mass SpectrometryMetabolomics Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.01724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01724) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Madina Bekbergenova, Lucas Pradi, Benjamin Navet, Emma Tysinger, Franck Michel, Matthieu Feraud, Yousouf Taghzouti, Yan Zhou Chen, Olivier Kirchhoffer, Florence Mehl, Martin Legrand, Tao Jiang, Marco Pagni, Soha Hassoun, Jean-Luc Wolfender, Wout Bittremieux, Fabien Gandon, Louis-Félix Nothias
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Knowledge Graph, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 问题：质谱代谢组学数据复杂，知识图谱统一了异构信息，但需要专业查询语言。方法：开源的基于LLM的多智能体框架MetaboT，将自然语言问题转换为SPARQL查询，包含范围验证、实体解析、模式感知查询生成、迭代精炼和结果解释模块。结果：在ENPKG基准上验证，能够回答有关植物-代谢物关系和生物活性的复杂问题。

<h3 id="382">[382] The Shape of Reasoning: Topological Analysis of Reasoning Traces in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.20665](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20665) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xue Wen Tan, Nathaniel Tan, Galen Lee, Stanley Kok
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Evaluation, Topological Data Analysis
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 问题：评估LLM推理轨迹质量劳动密集且不可靠，现有基于图的代理方法无法捕捉推理质量。方法：引入拓扑数据分析（TDA）框架，捕捉推理轨迹的几何结构，实现标签高效的自动评估。结果：拓扑特征在预测推理质量方面显著优于标准图度量；一组紧凑且稳定的拓扑特征可靠指示轨迹质量。

<h3 id="383">[383] SynthTools: A Framework for Scaling Synthetic Tools for Agent Development</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.09572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09572) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tommaso Castellani, Naimeng Ye, Daksh Mittal, Thomson Yen, Emmanouil Koukoumidis, William Zeng, Hongseok Namkoong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 问题：智能体系统需要大量多样且可控的工具使用环境。方法：SynthTools，全LLM管线，涵盖环境生成、仿真、验证和任务构建，包括自上而下的环境生成和自下而上的任务轨迹生成。结果：发布包含73,883个验证工具、6,800个环境和100个领域的数据集；在Qwen3模型上训练后，在多个工具使用基准上获得提升，包括真实API。

<h3 id="384">[384] Guaranteed Optimal Compositional Explanations for Neurons</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.20934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20934) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Biagio La Rosa, Leilani H. Gilpin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Mechanistic Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：神经元的组合解释通常使用束搜索，无法保证最优性。方法：提出首个在全部假设空间上计算保证最优组合解释的框架，包括分解、启发式算法和高效算法。结果：10-40%的先前解释在涉及重叠概念时非最优；基于分解的束搜索变体在运行时间上匹配或改进先前方法。

<h3 id="385">[385] Debate with Images: Detecting Deceptive Behaviors in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.00349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00349) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sitong Fang, Shiyi Hou, Kaile Wang, Boyuan Chen, Donghai Hong, Jiayi Zhou, Josef Dai, Yaodong Yang, Jiaming Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deception Detection, Multimodal Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了多模态大语言模型中的欺骗行为风险，并提出了首个多模态欺骗评估基准MM-DeceptionBench。针对现有评估方法的盲点，作者设计了基于图像辩论的多智能体监控框架。实验表明该方法在GPT-4o上将Cohen's kappa提升1.5倍，准确率提升1.25倍。

<h3 id="386">[386] Atomic Skills are the Prerequisite: When Reinforcement Learning Synthesizes Compositional Reasoning, and When It Only Amplifies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.01970](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01970) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sitao Cheng, Xunjian Yin, Ruiwen Zhou, Yuxuan Li, Xinyi Wang, Liangming Pan, William Yang Wang, Victor Zhong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Compositional Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Skill Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sitaocheng/from_atomic_to_composite)
- **Summary**: 论文研究强化学习在组合推理中是否只是放大已有技能还是合成新技能。通过构建受控合成数据集，将能力分解为参数推理和上下文推理，发现监督微调仅导致记忆，而强化学习仅在原子技能已掌握时才能有效泛化。结果表明解耦原子训练后使用强化学习可推广到复杂新颖推理。

<h3 id="387">[387] CircuitLM: A Multi-Agent LLM-Aided Design Framework for Generating Circuit Schematics from Natural Language Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.04505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04505) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Khandakar Shakib Al Hasan, Syed Rifat Raiyan, Hasin Mahtab Alvee, Wahid Sadik
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; eess.SY
- **Tags**: EDA, Circuit Design, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: ICLAD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Khandakar227/CircuitLM)
- **Summary**: 电路原理图生成中存在幻觉和违反物理约束的问题。论文提出CircuitLM多智能体流水线，通过组件知识库和链式推理生成结构化JSON原理图，并采用电气规则检查和元评估双重验证。实验证明该方法能有效生成可行的电路原理图。

<h3 id="388">[388] How Much Can a Few Engine Moves Help? Quantifying Limited Cheating in Chess</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.05386](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05386) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daniel Keren
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Game AI, Cheating Detection, Strategy Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE CoG 2026
- **Summary**: 针对象棋中有限次作弊的收益量化问题，论文提出了基于阈值和Bellman策略的干预方法，并在Stockfish引擎环境下测试。结果表明1-2次适时作弊可使平均得分从0.51提升至0.71和0.82。该工作旨在测量作弊效果以辅助检测。

<h3 id="389">[389] SONIC-O1: A Real-World Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Large Language Models on Audio-Video Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21666) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Y. Radwan, Christos Emmanouilidis, Hina Tabassum, Deval Pandya, Shaina Raza
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV

<h3 id="390">[390] Emergent Analogical Reasoning in Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01992) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Analogical Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability, Transformer Architecture
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 论文形式化类比推理为范畴论中的函子，并设计合成任务研究Transformer中类比推理的出现机制。通过机理分析发现该能力依赖于嵌入空间中的几何对齐和Transformer内部的函子应用，且受数据、优化和规模影响。该趋势也在预训练LLM中得到验证。

<h3 id="391">[391] Aligning Language Model Benchmarks with Pairwise Preferences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02898) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL

- **Summary**: 语言模型基准常无法预测实际效用。论文提出基准对齐方法BenchAlign，利用模型在问题上的表现和成对偏好自动更新基准权重。实验表明对齐后的基准能准确排序未见模型。

<h3 id="392">[392] Can I Have Your Order? Monte-Carlo Tree Search for Slot Filling Ordering in Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.12586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12586) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Joshua Ong Jun Leang, Yu Zhao, Mihaela Cătălina Stoian, Wenda Li, Shay B. Cohen, Eleonora Giunchiglia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Monte Carlo Tree Search, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 掩码扩散模型中的槽填充顺序严重影响输出质量。论文提出McDiffuSE框架，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索优化填充顺序，通过前瞻模拟探索组合空间。实验相比自回归基线提升3.2%，在MBPP和MATH500上分别提升19.5%和4.9%。

<h3 id="393">[393] AMA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Memory for Agentic Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.22769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22769) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yujie Zhao, Boqin Yuan, Junbo Huang, Haocheng Yuan, Zhongming Yu, Haozhou Xu, Lanxiang Hu, Abhilash Shankarampeta, Zimeng Huang, Wentao Ni, Yuandong Tian, Jishen Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Memory Architecture, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有记忆基准主要集中在对话场景，缺乏对智能体真实轨迹的评估。论文提出AMA-Bench基准和AMA-Agent记忆系统，基于因果关系图与工具增强检索。AMA-Agent达到57.22%准确率，超过最强基线11.16%。

<h3 id="394">[394] DIG to Heal: Scaling General-purpose Agent Collaboration via Explainable Dynamic Decision Paths</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.00309](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00309) (replaced)

<h3 id="395">[395] COOP$^2$: Defining, Observing, and Repairing Cooperation in LLM Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.00349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00349) (replaced)

<h3 id="396">[396] FinTexTS: Financial Text-Paired Time-Series Dataset via Semantic-Based and Multi-Level Pairing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02702) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaehoon Lee, Suhwan Park, Taeyoon Lim, Seunghan Lee, Jun Seo, Dongwan Kang, Hwanil Choi, Minjae Kim, Sungdong Yoo, Soonyoung Lee, Yongjae Lee, Wonbin Ahn
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Time Series Forecasting, Dataset
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 金融文本配对时间序列数据集常忽略复杂依赖关系。论文提出基于语义和多级配对的框架FinTexTS，通过嵌入匹配和LLM分类构建多层级新闻-股票配对。实验证明该策略在股价预测中有效。

<h3 id="397">[397] Towards automated data analysis: A guided framework for LLM-based risk estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04631) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Panteleimon Rodis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Analysis, Risk Analysis, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 数据集风险分析目前依赖耗时的手动审计，而完全自动化存在幻觉。论文提出一个基于LLM并在人类指导下进行风险估计的框架，通过识别模式、建议聚类、生成代码并解释结果。概念验证展示了可行性。

<h3 id="398">[398] CRaFT: Circuit-Guided Refusal Feature Selection via Cross-Layer Transcoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.01604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01604) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Su-Hyeon Kim, Hyundong Jin, Yejin Lee, Yo-Sub Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 现有拒绝特征选择方法仅依赖激活强度，易受表面启发式影响。论文提出CRaFT，利用跨层编码器构建稀疏特征电路图，通过聚合路径效应识别关键特征。在四个越狱基准上平均性能从6.7%提升至57.4%。

<h3 id="399">[399] FactReview: Evidence-Grounded Peer Review with Execution-Based Claim Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.04074](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04074) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ling Yue, Chaoqian Ouyang, Hang Xu, Ruijun Huang, Yuchen Liu, Libin Zheng, Wei Liu, Shaowu Pan, Shimin Di, Min-Ling Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Peer Review, LLM Application, Code Execution
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/FactReview)
- **Summary**: LLM审稿系统通常仅处理稿件，无法验证文献和代码声明。论文提出FactReview，提取声明并与相关工作关联，执行代码审计。在35篇论文上覆盖84%声明，审稿质量4.86/5，审稿时间减少58%。

<h3 id="400">[400] Graph-of-Skills: Dependency-Aware Structural Retrieval for Massive Agent Skills</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.05333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05333) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dawei Liu, Zongxia Li, Hongyang Du, Xiyang Wu, Shihang Gui, Yongbei Kuang, Lichao Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Graph Neural Network
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/davidliuk/graph-of-skills)
- **Summary**: 大规模技能库面临上下文饱和和前置技能缺失问题。论文提出技能图GoS，离线构建可执行技能图，在线通过混合搜索和反向感知PPR检索依赖完备的技能束。在SkillsBench上最高提升奖励25.55%，减少56.72%令牌。

<h3 id="401">[401] Text2Model: Modeling Copilots for Text-to-Model Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.12955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12955) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Serdar Kadioglu, Karthik Uppuluri, Akash Singirikonda
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Prompt Engineering, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对使用大语言模型将自然语言描述的优化和满足问题转化为正式模型这一任务，本文首次在统一架构和数据集下整合了满足和优化问题，并提出了求解器无关的方法。通过基于MiniZinc的建模能力，提出了多种策略的copilot套件Text2Model及跨领域数据集Text2Zinc。实验表明，这些策略在部分任务上达到或超越了近期研究，但大语言模型尚未成为组合建模的即按即用技术。

<h3 id="402">[402] Prompt Optimization Is a Coin Flip: Diagnosing When It Helps in Compound AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.14585](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14585) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xing Zhang, Guanghui Wang, Yanwei Cui, Wei Qiu, Ziyuan Li, Bing Zhu, Peiyang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, LLM Evaluation, Compound AI Systems
- **Venue**: CTB@ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文通过大量实验揭示在复合AI系统中，提示优化（如TextGrad、DSPy）的表现与抛硬币无异，49%的方案不如零样本。研究发现，优化仅在任务具有可利用的输出结构时才有帮助，并且交互效应不显著。作者提供了两阶段诊断方法，以预测优化是否值得。

<h3 id="403">[403] Towards Rigorous Explainability by Feature Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15898) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Olivier Létoffé, Xuanxiang Huang, Joao Marques-Silva
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Feature Attribution
- **Summary**: 本文概述了使用严格符号方法替代非严格非符号方法（如SHAP）进行特征归因解释的努力。重点讨论了在领域中，非符号方法缺乏严谨性，而符号方法可以提供更可靠的解释。

<h3 id="404">[404] OGER: A Robust Offline-Guided Exploration Reward for Hybrid Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18530](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18530) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Ma, Mingzhou Xu, Xuebo Liu, Chang Jin, Qiang Wang, Derek F. Wong, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Reward Design

<h3 id="405">[405] Escher-Loop: Mutual Evolution by Closed-Loop Self-Referential Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23472](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23472) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Liu, Xinyan Guo, Xuchen Wei, Han Hao, Liu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 本文提出Escher-Loop闭环框架，通过任务智能体和优化智能体的相互演化实现开放式改进。利用动态基准机制，将新任务智能体的得分作为相对胜负信号更新优化器。实验表明，该框架能突破静态基线性能上限，在数学优化问题上取得最高峰值性能。

<h3 id="406">[406] DataClawBench: An Agent Benchmark for Exploratory Real-World Financial Data Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02503) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiaohong Zhang, Weihao Ye, Jialong Chen, Yi Luo, BoYuan Li, Bowen Deng, Zibin Zheng, Jianhao Lin, Wei-Shi Zheng, Chuan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 针对现有基准低估探索负担的问题，本文提出DataClawBench，基于金融智库场景，要求智能体独立探索嘈杂的多领域数据并得出可验证结论。基准包含约206万条记录和492个多步跨领域任务，并标注中间里程碑以诊断探索和推理失败。评估表明，探索性数据分析破坏智能体可靠性。

<h3 id="407">[407] Can We Formally Verify Neural PDE Surrogates? SMT Compilation of Small Fourier Neural Operators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.08938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08938) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ali Baheri, Ignacio Laguna Peralta
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Operator, Neural Verification, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 本文研究对傅立叶神经算子（FNO）进行形式验证，利用其分段线性性质在Z3中精确编码。在10个小型FNO代理上，精确编码能够产生正定性证明和反例，但存在可扩展性问题。轻量级冻结编码可扩展至更大网格，但丧失了原始FNO的保证。

<h3 id="408">[408] Verifiable Process Rewards for Agentic Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10325](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10325) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Huining Yuan, Zelai Xu, Huaijie Wang, Xiangmin Yi, Jiaxuan Gao, Xiao-Ping Zhang, Yu Wang, Chao Yu, Yi Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Agent, Process Reward Model
- **Summary**: 针对长程智能体推理中稀疏奖励的信用分配问题，本文提出可验证过程奖励（VPR）框架，利用符号或算法神谕提供密集的回合级监督。理论分析和实验表明，VPR在数学推理和逻辑推理等基准上优于基于结果奖励和基于rollout的过程奖励基线，并迁移到通用推理任务。

<h3 id="409">[409] Adaptive Teacher Exposure for Self-Distillation in LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11458](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11458) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zihao Han, Tiangang Zhang, Huaibin Wang, Yilun Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation, Self-Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文指出现有自蒸馏方法中教师始终看到完整推理过程存在暴露不匹配问题，并提出ATESD方法，通过轻量级Beta控制器自适应调节教师暴露比例，并利用折扣学习进度奖励优化。在AIME 24等基准上，ATESD在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上持续优于竞争性Self-distillation和RL基线。

<h3 id="410">[410] Optimal LTLf Synthesis</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO

<h3 id="411">[411] Democratizing Large-Scale Re-Optimization with LLM-Guided Model Patches</h3>

<h3 id="412">[412] Position: The Turing-Completeness of Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19514](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guanyu Cui, Zhewei Wei, Kun He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Architecture, Transformer Architecture, Context Management
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Position Paper Track
- **Summary**: 本文区分了固定Transformer系统设置和缩放家族设置，指出现有的Transformer图灵完备性证明多在缩放家族设置中建立，而实际部署对应固定系统设置。作者主张上下文管理是决定实际自回归Transformer计算能力的关键因素，不同上下文管理方法导致不同的计算能力。

<h3 id="413">[413] EngiAI: A Multi-Agent Framework and Benchmark Suite for LLM-Driven Engineering Design</h3>

<h3 id="414">[414] Who Uses AI? Platform Selection and the Measurement of Occupational AI Exposure</h3>

<h3 id="415">[415] FLUID: From Ephemeral IDs to Multimodal Semantic Codes for Industrial-Scale Livestreaming Recommendation</h3>

<h3 id="416">[416] Adapting the Interface, Not the Model: Runtime Harness Adaptation for Deterministic LLM Agents</h3>

- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Debugging, Runtime Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Tianshi-Xu/Life-Harness)
- **Summary**: 本文提出Life-Harness运行时生命周期感知框架，通过从训练轨迹中转换重复交互失败为可复用干预，在不改变模型权重的情况下改进冻结LLM代理。在126个模型-环境设置中平均相对提升88.5%，且从Qwen3-4B-Instruct轨迹演化的harness可迁移到其他17个模型。

<h3 id="417">[417] In Search of the Ingredients of Open-Endedness: Replicating Picbreeder with Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23908) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sam Earle, Kai Arulkumaran, Andrew Dai, Akarsh Kumar, Julian Togelius, Sebastian Risi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Open-Ended Evolution, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: GECCO 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/smearle/picbreeder-vlm)
- **Summary**: 问题：人工代理是否能像人类一样进行开放式的发现？方法：用视觉语言模型复现Picbreeder，添加探索噪声、行为多样性和叙事记忆。结果：与人类基线存在定性差异，使用系统发育复杂度和视觉语义显著性等指标量化。

<h3 id="418">[418] From Accuracy to Auditability: A Survey of Determinism in Financial AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.23955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23955) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruizhe Zhou, Xiaoyang Liu, Gaoyuan Du, Yi Zheng, Shouxi Ren, Deepayan Chakrabarti, Dengdu Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LG; cs.SI; q-fin.CP
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 问题：金融AI系统中由于硬件和架构导致的机械非确定性和可重复性问题。方法：调研并实验量化了表格模型、图网络和LLM工作流中的解释排名不稳定性、预测翻转率和输出差异。结果：提出了分层评估框架，使用RBO、D_cos、TDI、PSD等指标评估可审计性。

<h3 id="419">[419] Exploration of Perceptual Speech Features for Clinical Decision-Support in Mental Health Care</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24678) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vassilis Lyberatos, Edmund G. Dervakos, Eleni Adamidi, Athanasios Voulodimos, Giorgos Stamou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Mental Health, Clinical Decision Support
- **Venue**: CLPsych 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：心理健康评估需要客观可解释的语音线索。方法：基于韵律、声音质量、语义连贯性等特征，使用XGBoost与SHAP和LIME进行统计分析。结果：症状严重性与声音不规则性（shimmer、jitter）、词汇句法模式和情感语调之间存在稳定关联。

<h3 id="420">[420] Boosting Inference with Guided Reasoning: Stochastic Exploration for Recursive Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25230) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrew Corbett, Archit Sood, Anna Tzatzopoulou, Sai-Aakash Ramesh, Tim Dodwell
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Probabilistic Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 问题：递归架构的推理时行为理解不足。方法：通过随机扰动提出大致推理轨迹，并使用提前停止头在线重加权。结果：在Sudoku-Extreme上将精确解准确率从85.9%提升至98.0%，无需重新训练；诊断工具可预测改进效果。

<h3 id="421">[421] Behind EvoMap: Characterizing a Self-Evolving Agent-to-Agent Collaboration Network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25815) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiming Ye, Peixain Zhang, Yupeng He, Zifan Peng, Gareth Tyson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Incentive Mechanism
- **Summary**: 问题：去中心化代理协作网络（A2A）在实际中的运作方式。方法：对EvoMap网络进行大规模实证研究，分析150万资产和12.8万代理。结果：设计选择导致98%资产从未被复用，奖励集中；评分系统存在缺陷，84%通过验证的资产使用空测试。

<h3 id="422">[422] MobileGym: A Verifiable and Highly Parallel Simulation Platform for Mobile GUI Agent Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26114) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dingbang Wu, Rui Hao, Haiyang Wang, Shuzhe Wu, Han Xiao, Zhenghong Li, Bojiang Zhou, Zheng Ju, Zichen Liu, Lue Fan, Zhaoxiang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Reinforcement Learning, Mobile AI
- **Summary**: 问题：移动GUI代理研究缺乏可验证、可扩展的模拟平台。方法：MobileGym，一个轻量级浏览器托管环境，支持确定性状态判断和低成本并行回滚（每实例约400MB，3秒冷启动）。结果：MobileGym-Bench包含416个任务；GRPO训练在Qwen3-VL-4B上提升12.8个百分点，真实设备保留95.1%的训练增益。

<h3 id="423">[423] Measuring Massive Multitask Chinese Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2304.12986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.12986) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hui Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Multilingual Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：大规模中文语言模型缺乏能力评估。方法：提出涵盖医学、法律、心理学和教育等领域的多任务测试。结果：零样本设置下最佳模型平均高出最差模型18.6个百分点；最高平均准确率0.512；法律领域表现最差（0.239）。

<h3 id="424">[424] DSSE: a drone swarm search environment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2307.06240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.06240) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Manuel Castanares, Luis F. S. Carrete, Enrico F. Damiani, Leonardo D. M. de Abreu, José Fernando B. Brancalion, Fabrício J. Barth
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, UAV, Simulation
- **Venue**: JOSS
- **Summary**: 问题：需要研究动态概率输入的强化学习环境，用于无人机群搜索。方法：基于PettingZoo的DSSE环境，代理（无人机）使用概率地图寻找目标。结果：环境设计用于研究需要动态概率输入的RL算法。

<h3 id="425">[425] Delay-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Highway On-Ramp Merging under Stochastic Communication Latency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.11852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11852) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Amin Tabrizian, Zhitong Huang, Arsyi Aziz, Peng Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Reinforcement Learning, V2X
- **Summary**: 问题：V2I通信导致的随机延迟破坏了强化学习在高速公路并道中的马尔可夫假设。方法：DAROM框架，建模为随机延迟MDP，使用延迟感知编码器恢复马尔可夫表示，并集成物理安全控制器。结果：GRU编码器在高密度交通中实现超过99%的成功率，容忍最高2.0秒随机延迟。

<h3 id="426">[426] Generalized Holographic Reduced Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2405.09689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.09689) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Calvin Yeung, Zhuowen Zou, SungHeon Jeong, Wenjun Huang, Nathaniel D Bastian, Mohsen Imani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Hyperdimensional Computing, Attention Mechanism, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：超维计算（HDC）的绑定操作在处理复杂组合结构时能力有限。方法：提出广义全息约简表示（GHRR），具有非交换绑定操作，可实现注意力机制。结果：GHRR绑定更具表现力；用GHRR等价物替换Transformer中的注意力机制，在语言建模任务上表现更优。

<h3 id="427">[427] Sinc Kolmogorov-Arnold network and its application for solving PDEs with singularities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.04096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04096) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianchi Yu, Jingwei Qiu, Jiang Yang, Ivan Oseledets
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; math.NA
- **Tags**: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks, Physics-Informed Learning, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 问题：KAN需要能有效表示具有奇异点的函数的激活函数。方法：使用Sinc插值构建SincKAN。结果：在函数逼近和偏微分方程求解中，SincKAN在几乎所有示例中表现更好。

<h3 id="428">[428] Revisiting Graph Autoencoders as Implicit Contrastive Learners</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.10241](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10241) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jintang Li, Ruofan Wu, Yuchang Zhu, Huizhe Zhang, Zulun Zhu, Liang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EdisonLeeeee/lrGAE)
- **Summary**: 问题：图自编码器（GAE）和图对比学习（GCL）通常被孤立研究。方法：将GAE视为隐式图对比学习器，提出统一框架强调非对称对比视图。结果：系统实验表明该视角能更清晰理解现有模型并指导设计有效且可扩展的GAE。

<h3 id="429">[429] Isometry pursuit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.18502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18502) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Samson Koelle, Marina Meila
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Optimization, Representation Learning, Isometry Pursuit
- **Summary**: 问题：如何从宽矩阵中识别正交列子矩阵。方法：等距追踪算法，包括新颖的归一化方法和多任务基追踪。结果：理论和实验结果证明该方法有效，为坐标选择和多样化提供了协同替代方案。

<h3 id="430">[430] Improving Requirements Classification with SMOTE-Tomek Preprocessing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.06491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06491) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Barak Or
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Text Classification, Class Imbalance
- **Summary**: 问题：需求分类中类别不平衡问题。方法：使用SMOTE-Tomek预处理和分层K折交叉验证。结果：逻辑回归准确率达到76.16%，显著超过基线58.31%。

<h3 id="431">[431] HEART: Achieving Timely Multi-Model Training for Vehicle-Edge-Cloud-Integrated Hierarchical Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.09934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09934) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaohong Yang, Minghui Liwang, Xianbin Wang, Zhipeng Cheng, Seyyedali Hosseinalipour, Huaiyu Dai, Zhenzhen Jiao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Edge Computing, Multi-Task Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE Transactions on Cloud Computing
- **Summary**: 问题：车辆-边缘-云分层联邦学习中的多模型训练挑战（模型过时、移动性、资源分配）。方法：HEART框架，混合同步-异步聚合，改进的粒子群和遗传算法用于调度，贪心算法确定优先级。结果：在真实数据集上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="432">[432] Beyond External Monitors: Enhancing Transparency of Large Language Models for Easier Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.05242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05242) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guanxu Chen, Jing Shao, Tao Luo, Lijie Hu, Qihao Lin, Dongrui Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, LLM Safety, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM的思维过程不透明，CoT不能准确反映真实推理。方法：TELLME方法提高LLM透明度。结果：在去毒化任务中跨模态测试集、不同架构和参数规模取得一致改进；通过最优传输理论分析泛化能力。

<h3 id="433">[433] MCTS-Judge: Test-Time Scaling in LLM-as-a-Judge for Code Correctness Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.12468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12468) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yutong Wang, Pengliang Ji, Chaoqun Yang, Kaixin Li, Ming Hu, Jiaoyang Li, Guillaume Sartoretti
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Code Generation, Monte Carlo Tree Search
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM-as-a-Judge在代码正确性评估中可靠性不足的问题，提出了MCTS-Judge框架，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索进行测试时缩放，将问题分解为多角度评估。实验表明，该方法将基础模型准确率从41%提升至80%，且推理token数比o1系列减少3倍。

<h3 id="434">[434] GradientStabilizer:Fix the Norm, Not the Gradient</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.17055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17055) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianjin Huang, Zhangyang Wang, Haotian Hu, Zhenyu Zhang, Gaojie Jin, Xiang Li, Li Shen, Jiaxing Shang, Tianlong Chen, Ke Li, Lu Liu, Qingsong Wen, Shiwei Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, LLM Training, Gradient Stabilization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对训练不稳定性由梯度范数尖峰引起的问题，提出GradientStabilizer，一种轻量级梯度变换，用稳定估计替代更新幅度，保留梯度方向。在LLM预训练、量化感知训练等任务中，该方法提高了稳定性，扩大了稳定学习率区间，减少了发散。

<h3 id="435">[435] MM-PoisonRAG: Disrupting Multimodal RAG with Local and Global Poisoning Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.17832](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17832) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hyeonjeong Ha, Qiusi Zhan, Jeonghwan Kim, Dimitrios Bralios, Saikrishna Sanniboina, Nanyun Peng, Kai-Wei Chang, Daniel Kang, Heng Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.CV
- **Tags**: RAG, Multimodal Learning, Data Poisoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HyeonjeongHa/MM-PoisonRAG)
- **Summary**: 本文研究多模态RAG系统在知识投毒攻击下的脆弱性，提出MM-PoisonRAG框架，包括局部和全局投毒攻击策略。实验表明，局部攻击成功率可达56%，全局攻击单次投毒即可将生成准确率降至0%，且能绕过现有防御。

<h3 id="436">[436] Persuade Me if You Can: A Framework for Evaluating Persuasion Effectiveness and Susceptibility Among Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.01829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01829) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nimet Beyza Bozdag, Shuhaib Mehri, Gokhan Tur, Dilek Hakkani-Tür
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, AI Persuasion
- **Venue**: ACM Conference on AI and Agentic Systems 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM的劝说能力及其易受影响性，提出PMIYC自动框架用于多智能体交互中的劝说评估。实验发现Llama-3.3-70B和GPT-4o劝说效果相当，但GPT-4o对错误信息的抵抗力比Llama-3.3-70B高50%以上。

<h3 id="437">[437] Deepfake-Eval-2024: A Multi-Modal In-the-Wild Benchmark of Deepfakes Circulated in 2024</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.02857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02857) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nuria Alina Chandra, Hannah Lee, Ryan Murtfeldt, Lin Qiu, Arnab Karmakar, Emmanuel Tanumihardja, Kevin Farhat, Ben Caffee, Changyeon Lee, Jongwook Choi, Sejin Paik, Aerin Kim, Oren Etzioni
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Benchmark, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nuriachandra/Deepfake-Eval-2024)
- **Summary**: 本文指出现有深度伪造检测基准过时，提出Deepfake-Eval-2024基准，包含2024年社交媒体的45小时视频、56.5小时音频和1975张图像。评估发现开源模型AUC相比旧基准下降50%（视频）、48%（音频）、45%（图像）。

<h3 id="438">[438] Manboformer: Learning Gaussian Representations via Spatial-temporal Attention Mechanism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.04863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04863) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyue Zhao, Qining Qi, Jianfa Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, 3D Gaussian Splatting, Spatiotemporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文针对3D语义占用预测中GaussianFormer性能受限问题，提出Manboformer，利用时空自注意力机制优化高斯表示。实验基于NuScenes数据集，但论文因缺陷已撤回。

<h3 id="439">[439] The Point, the Vision and the Text: Does Point Cloud Boost Spatial Reasoning of Large Language Models? A Bias-Controlled Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.04540](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04540) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weichen Zhang, Ruiying Peng, Xin Zeng, Jianjie Fang, Ziyou Wang, Kaiyuan Li, Heng Dong, Wei Li, Chen Gao, Xin Wang, Xinlei Chen, Yong Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spatial Reasoning, Point Cloud, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EmbodiedCity/ScanReQA.code)
- **Summary**: 本文探究点云是否增强LLM的空间推理能力，提出ScanReQA基准。实验发现基于点云和视觉的多模态LLM推理能力优于纯文本LLM，但二元空间推理仍有挑战，且3D LLM存在注意力沉没问题。

<h3 id="440">[440] LiDDA: Data Driven Attribution at LinkedIn</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.09861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09861) (replaced)
- **Authors**: John Bencina, Erkut Aykutlug, Yue Chen, Zerui Zhang, Stephanie Sorenson, Shao Tang, Changshuai Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Attribution, Marketing Analytics, Data-Driven Attribution
- **Summary**: 本文针对营销归因问题，提出统一的基于Transformer的归因方法，可处理成员级和聚合级数据及外部宏观因素。在LinkedIn大规模部署，展示了显著的业务影响。

<h3 id="441">[441] Structured Agent Distillation for Large Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.13820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13820) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jun Liu, Zhenglun Kong, Peiyan Dong, Changdi Yang, Tianqi Li, Hao Tang, Geng Yuan, Wei Niu, Wenbin Zhang, Pu Zhao, Xue Lin, Dong Huang, Yanzhi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Knowledge Distillation, Agent Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM智能体推理成本高的问题，提出结构化智能体蒸馏框架，将轨迹分段为[REASON]和[ACT]并施加分段损失。在ALFWorld等任务上，该方法优于词级别蒸馏基线，实现有效压缩且性能下降小。

<h3 id="442">[442] EVADE-Bench: Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating and Enhancing Evasive Content Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.17654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17654) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ancheng Xu, Zhihao Yang, Jingpeng Li, Guanghu Yuan, Longze Chen, Liang Yan, Jiehui Zhou, Zhen Qin, Hengyu Chang, Yukun Chen, Hamid Alinejad-Rokny, Min Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Benchmark, E-Commerce
- **Venue**: SIGIR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对电商中规避性内容检测难题，提出EVADE-Bench多模态基准。评估26个模型发现即使最先进模型也常误分类规避样本，而多智能体分解策略可显著提升准确率。

<h3 id="443">[443] STFlow: Data-Coupled Flow Matching for Geometric Trajectory Simulation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
